*Chang Xia,Leilei Ouyang,Huimin Wang,Yong Zhao,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 Debate-Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA) 的多智能体框架，用于解决大语言模型在临床诊断中的单轮问答局限性。在罕见病和复杂病例数据集上，DMoA 显著提升了诊断准确率和安全性，且消融实验证实了结构化工作流的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在医疗任务中展现潜力，但其单轮问答格式无法反映实际临床诊断的迭代推理过程，导致在复杂诊断场景中表现受限。

Method: 开发了 Debate-Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA) 框架，通过基于角色的结构化交互支持迭代诊断推理。在 297 例罕见病病例和 1,719 例疑难病例上评估基础模型和 DMoA，并进行消融实验分析框架设计、基础模型选择和 token 预算对性能的影响。

Result: 在两个数据集上，DMoA 将最可能诊断准确率提升了 10.21 个百分点，安全率提升了 11.36 个百分点（相较于 GPT-4o 基线）。消融实验表明提升不仅源于使用更多模型或更长输出，还反映了结构化工作流的贡献。进一步分析显示：4*2 结构、更强基础模型和更大 token 预算下性能更优。

Conclusion: DMoA 证明了多智能体框架在临床任务中的潜力，建议进一步研究此类框架以改进复杂医疗决策支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show potential for medical tasks, but their single-turn question-answer format does not reflect how clinical diagnosis is performed in practice. As a result, they remain limited in complex diagnostic settings. We developed Debate-Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA), a novel multi-agent framework that structures role-based interaction to support iterative diagnostic reasoning. Base models and DMoA were evaluated on 297 rare disease cases and 1,719 challenging cases. Across both datasets, DMoA improved most likely diagnosis accuracy by 10.21 percentage points and safety rate by 11.36 percentage points over GPT-4o baseline. Ablation experiments showed that the gains were not simply due to the use of more models or longer outputs, but also reflected the contribution of the structured workflow. Further analyses examined how framework design, base model choice, and token budget affected performance. DMoA performed better with a 4*2 structure, stronger base models, and a larger token budget. These findings demonstrate the potential of DMoA for clinical tasks and suggest further investigation of multi-agent frameworks.

</details>


### [53] [Influence Score and Transformers interpretability: Measure of the Effective Impact of Attention Heads at inference time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05074)
*Lisa Bouger,Yannick Teglia,Philippe Loubet Moundi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种影响力评分，量化注意力头在提示词注入检测Transformer模型分类决策中的贡献，结合logits方向影响与残差流结构贡献，实现头、层、网络多尺度分析，揭示正确与错误预测的不同决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在细粒度电路分析与全局输出分析之间存在权衡，缺乏系统性方法研究Transformer分类器的决策机制，特别是针对提示词注入检测任务需要解释性更强的分析工具。

Method: 设计影响力评分，融合注意力头对logits的方向性影响及其在残差流中的结构贡献，支持在头、层、网络三个层面进行多尺度分析；将该框架应用于专门用于提示词注入检测的DeBERTa模型。

Result: 实验显示框架能区分正确预测与错误预测的决策行为模式，揭示不同层级注意力头的贡献差异。

Conclusion: 该方法在细粒度电路分析与全局输出分析之间提供有效折中，为系统性研究Transformer分类器的决策机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose an influence score to quantify the contribution of attention heads to classification decisions in Transformer-based models designed for prompt injection detection. The score combines directional influence on the logits with structural contribution within the residual stream, enabling a multi-scale analysis at the head, layer, and network levels. Applied to a DeBERTa model specialized for prompt injection detection, our framework reveals distinct decision behaviours between correct and erroneous predictions. Our method provides an effective compromise between fine-grained circuit analysis and global output-based methods, and offers a systematic way to study decision mechanisms in Transformer classifiers.

</details>


### [54] [Improving Language Identification for Code-Switched Utterances with Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05099)
*Joanna Radoła,Josep Maria Crego,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了无需训练的代码切换识别方法 MaskLID，发现并解决了其过度依赖词级语言关联分数的问题，将底层优化算法重构为整数线性规划（ILP），引入可解释的约束条件，在10种多样化语言的实验中显著提升了代码切换基准测试的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码切换话语的自动识别仍是语言识别系统的难题，导致此类文本在大语言模型训练数据中表现不足。现有的最佳方法 MaskLID 虽然无需训练且能检测任意语言组合，但存在过度依赖词级语言关联分数的主要缺陷，限制了其准确性。

Method: 作者提出三项改进：(a) 揭示并解决了 MaskLID 过度依赖词级语言关联分数的问题；(b) 将底层优化算法重构为整数线性规划（ILP），使得可以引入一大组清晰且可解释的约束条件；(c) 在包含10种多样化语言的实验中验证了每项改进的有效性。

Result: 每项改进均大幅提升了基线系统性能。在涉及10种多样化语言的代码切换基准测试实验中，观察到性能显著增强。同时发布了代码和数据以保证可复现性。

Conclusion: 通过将 MaskLID 的优化框架重构为整数线性规划并引入可解释约束，成功解决了其过度依赖词级语言关联分数的核心缺陷，显著提升了代码切换识别性能，为无监督代码切换语言识别提供了更稳健且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic identification of code-switched (CS) utterances remains a challenge for language identification (LID) systems, causing such texts to be underrepresented in the training data of Large Language Models. In this paper, we revisit MaskLID, a state-of-the art approach for CS identification, which requires no training and detects arbitrary language combinations. We make three main contributions: (a) we reveal, and address, a major issue of MaskLID: its overreliance on word-level language association scores; (b) we reformulate the underlying optimization algorithm as an Integer Linear Program, enabling us to experiment with a large set of clear and interpretable constraints; (c) each of these improvements vastly improves the baseline system, as we illustrate in experiments involving 10~diverse languages, where we observe a strong boost in performance on CS benchmarks. We release our code and data for reproducibility.

</details>


### [55] [NS-ST-GraphRAG: Neuro-Symbolic Spatio-Temporal GraphRAG for Literary Knowledge Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05139)
*Zheng Kui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-ST-GraphRAG，一种神经符号时空图增强生成框架，用于长篇文学叙事的检索增强生成，并发布Red-Chamber-QA基准。实验显示该框架在机械答案复现和语义判断准确率上优于基线，验证了时空图表示、约束抽取和可审计评估的集成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇文学叙事给检索增强生成带来独特挑战：相关证据分散跨章节、关系随叙事时间演变、正确答案可能联合依赖时空关系约束。现有方法缺乏对时空维度的显式建模和可追溯证据支持。

Method: 提出NS-ST-GraphRAG框架，集成本体指导抽取、确定性约束检查、双重时间坐标、空间场景属性和动态子图检索。不从单一语料库级图检索，而是选择查询时空范围内有效的图状态，并将生成答案锚定在可追溯证据上。同时构建Red-Chamber-QA基准，包含时、空、一般问题类别、分部分证据跨度和确定性捷径控制。

Result: 在120题保留测试集上，NS-ST-GraphRAG机械答案复现率0.733，优于冻结窗口基线0.675和闭卷模型0.083（McNemar精确检验p=0.092，方向有利但不显著）；语义裁判准确率0.866对比0.850。预设的H2约束类别条件未获支持。

Conclusion: 结果展示了时空图表示、约束抽取和可审计评估如何整合为统一框架，实现长篇叙事上的可验证知识处理。

Abstract: Long-form literary narratives pose a distinctive information-processing challenge for retrieval-augmented generation: relevant evidence is distributed across chapters, relations evolve over narrative time, and correct answers may depend jointly on temporal, spatial, and relational constraints. We propose NS-ST-GraphRAG, a neuro-symbolic spatio-temporal GraphRAG framework that integrates ontology-guided extraction, deterministic constraint checking, dual temporal coordinates, spatial scene attributes, and dynamic sub-graph retrieval. Instead of retrieving from a single corpus-level graph, the framework selects the graph state valid for the temporal and spatial scope of a query and grounds generated answers in traceable evidence. We further introduce Red-Chamber-QA, to our knowledge the first open multi-hop question-answering benchmark for classical Chinese literature, with time-, space-, and general-question categories, per-part evidence spans, and deterministic shortcut controls. On a 120-question held-out split, NS-ST-GraphRAG achieves mechanical answer reproduction of 0.733 versus 0.675 for the frozen window baseline and 0.083 for a closed-book model (McNemar exact p = 0.092, directionally favorable but not significant); semantic-judge accuracy is 0.866 versus 0.850. The pre-specified constrained-category condition of H2 is not supported by the delivered comparison. These results show how temporal graph representation, constrained extraction, and auditable evaluation integrate into a unified framework for verifiable knowledge processing over long-form narrative.

</details>


### [56] [A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for AI-Assisted Scoring in Large-Scale Writing Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05143)
*María Eugenia Curi,Germán Capdehourat,Isabel Amigo,Magdalena Romano,Rosana Serra,Adrián Silveira,Andrés Peri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个基于大语言模型的AI辅助评分框架，用于大规模国家考试的短文本评分（约150-200词）。研究采用人机协作策略，在真实操作环境中利用两届全国测试数据（各约5000份回答）评估AI与人工评分的一致性及对通过/不通过判定的影响。结果显示多数维度达中高度一致，证实AI辅助评分可行，但需配合精心设计的人工监督机制方能安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人工智能（特别是大语言模型）引入教育评估，旨在提升大规模评分的效率与可扩展性，同时保持评估质量。现有研究多局限于实验室环境，缺乏在真实国家级大规模考试中的验证，且需解决AI评分可靠性与人工复核资源分配的平衡问题。

Method: 设计包含人机协作决策流程的AI辅助评分框架，针对短文本回答。使用两届全国测试真实数据（各约5000份学生回答），跨多个评分量表维度分析AI生成分数与人工评分员的一致性，并评估决策流程对通过/不通过结果的影响。采用修正工作流识别最需人工复核的案例，优化专家资源配置。

Result: AI模型与人工评分在大多数量表维度上达成中等到高度一致，支持AI辅助评分在此场景下的可行性。提出的修正工作流能有效识别高价值人工复核案例，显著提升专家工作分配效率。研究证实仅当结合精心设计的人工监督时，AI辅助评分才能安全整合至大规模评估流程。

Conclusion: AI辅助评分在大规模国家考试中具有应用潜力，但必须建立严格的人机协作监督机制。未来研究方向包括：纵向监测模型-人工一致性的长期变化，分析AI辅助复核工作流可能引入的认知偏差，以及探索更稳健的部署策略。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI), particularly large language models (LLMs), into educational assessment has opened new opportunities to enhance the efficiency and scalability of grading processes. This study presents the design and validation of an AI-assisted scoring framework for written responses in a large-scale national assessment. The proposed approach focuses on short written texts of approximately 150-200 words and incorporates a human-in-the-loop strategy to preserve assessment quality while reducing manual workload. The study is grounded in a real operational context, using data from two recent editions of a nationwide test, each comprising approximately 5,000 student responses. We analyze the alignment between AI-generated scores and human raters across multiple rubric dimensions, as well as the impact of the proposed decision flow on pass/fail outcomes. Results show moderate to high agreement between the model and human evaluations in most dimensions, supporting the feasibility of AI assistance in this setting. Moreover, the proposed correction workflow identifies cases where human review is most valuable, enabling a more efficient allocation of expert effort. The findings suggest that AI-assisted scoring can be safely integrated into large-scale assessment processes only when combined with carefully designed human oversight. The paper concludes by discussing practical implications for deployment in national assessment systems and outlining future research directions, including longitudinal monitoring of model-human alignment and the analysis of potential cognitive bias introduced by AI-supported review workflows.

</details>


### [57] [From Vision to Language: Investigating Causal Information Flow in Multimodal Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05149)
*Davide Testa,Hugh Mee Wong,Alessandro Lenci,Bernardo Magnini,Albert Gatt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过层级因果干预分析视频-文本注意力路径，揭示视觉信息主要在候选答案选项处理阶段整合，名词作为语义锚点，动词用于时序关系，且发现VLM在时序推理上存在重构序列信息困难，可能受语言偏见影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估主要关注最终预测，缺乏对视觉证据如何支撑语言决策的理解，需追踪跨模态信息流以验证决策是否建立在视觉基础上。

Method: 在视频生成式多项选择设定下，对视频-文本注意力路径实施层级因果干预，针对空间、因果和时序视觉推理进行分析。

Result: 视觉信息主要在模型处理候选答案选项时整合，选项为最终决策的主要文本锚定点；名词在多模态丰富中充当语义锚点，动词在时序关系处理中更相关；时序推理呈现特异模式，VLM难以跨帧重构序列信息，但该脆弱性可能源于定义事件间时序关系的特定时序表达的语言偏见。

Conclusion: 视觉信息整合集中在答案选项处理阶段，词性差异反映多模态交互机制，时序推理缺陷可能部分归因于语言偏见而非纯视觉推理能力不足，提示未来需区分视觉与语言因素以改进VLM评估。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are commonly evaluated through their final predictions, but understanding whether these decisions are grounded in visual evidence requires tracing how visual information contributes to language-based decisions. With this purpose in mind, we investigate cross-modal information flow in a video-based generative multiple-choice-like setting by applying a layer-wise causal intervention on video-text attention pathways. We target spatial, causal, and temporal visual reasoning. Our results show that visual information is mainly integrated while the model processes the candidate answer options, which serve as the primary textual grounding sites for the final decision. We further show that nouns play an important role as semantic anchors during multimodal enrichment, while verbs are more relevant when temporal relations are processed. Finally, we identify a distinct pattern in temporal reasoning, suggesting that VLMs struggle to reconstruct sequential information across video frames, but we remark that such fragility may also reflect linguistic biases associated with specific temporal expressions used for defining the relation between events within a scene.

</details>


### [58] [Compression Beyond the Uncompressed: A Two-Stage Training Recipe for Soft Context Compression in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05152)
*Shuyu Guo,Shuo Zhang,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DEX-Comp 提出了一种两阶段训练配方（纯蒸馏+困难探索），用于软上下文压缩，实现 16× 压缩率和 4×-24× 推理加速，同时性能达到或超越未压缩的 RAG 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软上下文压缩方法通过从未压缩 RAG 系统蒸馏输出进行训练，固有地将其性能上限限制在原始模型水平，无法超越未压缩基线。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段纯蒸馏，仅在未压缩 RAG 正确回答的查询上预热压缩模型；第二阶段困难探索，仅在未压缩 RAG 失败的查询上运行强化学习，迫使模型探索更适合压缩表示的计算模式。

Result: 在 5 个开放域 QA 基准上（检索深度 top-5 到 top-30），实现 16× 上下文压缩，推理加速 4×-24×，性能在各检索深度上均达到或超过未压缩 RAG 基线。消融实验验证了各阶段贡献，并确认了跨数据集和骨干网络的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入困难探索阶段，DEX-Comp 成功打破了蒸馏式压缩的性能天花板，使压缩模型能够超越未压缩基线，同时保持显著的效率提升，为 RAG 系统的实用化部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances language models with external knowledge, but the lengthy retrieved context inflates the input and degrades inference efficiency. Soft context compression encodes each document into a substantially shorter embedding sequence. However, most existing approaches are trained by distilling outputs from uncompressed RAG systems, inherently limiting their performance relative to the original model. To address this limitation, we propose DEX-Comp, a two-stage training recipe: Pure Distillation warm-starts the compression model on the uncompressed RAG's correct responses only, and Hard Exploration then runs reinforcement learning solely on queries the uncompressed RAG fails, forcing the model to explore computation patterns better suited to compressed representations. On five open-domain QA benchmarks at retrieval depths from top-5 to top-30, DEX-Comp compresses retrieved contexts by $16\times$ and accelerates inference by $4\times$--$24\times$, while achieving performance comparable to or exceeding the uncompressed RAG baseline across retrieval depths. Ablations and evaluations across diverse datasets and backbones further confirm the contribution of each stage and the generalization of our approach.

</details>


### [59] [Can Large Language Models Anticipate Behavioral Responses to Social Policies? A Case of Pension Enrollment Prediction among China's Flexible Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05189)
*Yumiao Li,Peixin Liu,Donglin Di,Chen Li,Runhuan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexPension-LLM，首个针对中国灵活就业人员分层养老保险参保预测的领域专用大语言模型，结合DKI-RDistill方法（策略接地提示注入+ LoRA/SFT蒸馏+教师纠错），在CHFS 2019盲测集上达到0.9316 Composite F1，超越Claude Sonnet 4.5及15/17基线，与Claude Opus 4.6无统计学差距；外部四调查平均0.7549且性能波动最小。核心增益来自策略接地线索注入与错误过滤监督，推理轨迹可核验政策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会政策影响评估面临计量经济学外推不可靠、实地试点成本高昂的双重困境，急需低成本、高泛化的政策评估工具；现有通用LLM缺乏领域专业知识与政策规则接地能力，难以直接用于分层养老保险参保等复杂决策预测。

Method: 1. 构建FlexPension-LLM：基于开放权重MoE学生模型，经LoRA/SFT蒸馏。2. DKI-RDistill流程：① 策略接地提示注入——将Probit边际效应、户籍-省份养老规则等政策线索注入提示；② 理由增强监督——教师模型生成带推理轨迹的标签；③ 错误过滤纠错——对教师错误样本在真实标签下重新生成；④ 蒸馏至学生模型。

Result: CHFS 2019盲测：Composite F1 0.9316，超越Claude Sonnet 4.5及15/17基线，与Claude Opus 4.6无显著差异。四外部调查平均Composite F1 0.7549，最强系统中性能波动最窄。消融实验证实主要增益源自策略接地线索注入与错误过滤监督；推理轨迹可对照政策规则核验决策依据。

Conclusion: 领域专用LLM结合政策知识注入与错误过滤蒸馏，可在低成本下实现高精度、可解释的社会政策影响预测，为替代昂贵实地试点与不可靠外推提供可行范式；方法可迁移至其他分层政策决策任务。

Abstract: Assessing the impacts of social policy changes is a widely acknowledged challenge for policymakers. Econometric methods can be unreliable when extrapolating to hypothetical scenarios, while field pilot programs are highly costly. In this paper, we propose using large language models (LLMs) as policy-assessment tools adapted from general-purpose models. We present FlexPension-LLM, the first domain-specialized large language model for a hierarchical pension-enrollment prediction task among flexible workers in China, and introduce DKI-RDistill, which injects policy-grounded cues into the prompt, including Probit-derived marginal effects and hukou-province pension rules. The method then uses LoRA/SFT to distill rationale-augmented supervision into an open-weight MoE student, with teacher errors corrected by regenerating those cases under ground-truth labels. On a CHFS 2019 blind split, FlexPension-LLM achieves 0.9316 Composite F1, surpassing its Claude Sonnet 4.5 teacher and 15 of 17 baselines, and is statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.6. Across four external surveys, it averages 0.7549 Composite F1 and shows the narrowest performance range among the strongest systems. Component analysis shows that gains come mainly from policy-grounded cue injection and error-filtered supervision, while rationales provide decision traces that can be checked against policy rules.

</details>


### [60] [A Verifier-Guided Explainable Reasoning Framework with Gold-Anchored QLoRA, Task-Aware Mixture-of-Experts, and Group-Relative RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05221)
*Thi Kim Trang Vo,Nam Tien Le,Thi Kim Nguyet Vo,Minh Khang Tran,Duy Phuong Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个由验证器引导的可解释推理框架，用于透明的教育问答。该框架结合了锚定权威答案的QLoRA微调、任务感知的符号路由和组相对的RLVR强化学习。实验表明，RLVR主要提升推理结构的显式性（P3从50.68%提升至72.20%），而符号验证在系统层面补充了神经策略，提高答案可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然展现出强推理能力，但其生成的解释往往不一致、缺乏依据或难以验证。现有方法难以同时保证答案正确性、证据一致性和推理深度，尤其在教育问答等需要透明推理的场景中。因此需要一种结合神经网络与符号验证的框架，实现可验证、可解释的推理过程。

Method: 1. 使用字段加权的QLoRA将Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct锚定到权威答案进行监督微调。2. 轻量级路由器将逻辑问题分配给FOL/Z3验证器，物理问题分配给公式和单位感知的符号求解器。3. 验证器反馈用于候选评估、自我修正和RLVR奖励构建。4. 候选回答在三个维度评估：P1答案正确性、P2证据/单位一致性、P3推理深度与可解释性。5. 推理时采用无金标准的自一致性聚合多个候选，再由可选的仅问题物理验证器进行保守的系统级修正。

Result: 在438个保留样本上，RLVR将P3从50.68%提升至72.20%，混合P1保持稳定约55.94%。自一致性将仅模型P1从48.86%提升至50.23%，符号验证提供了剩余的混合增益。结果表明RLVR主要强化显式推理结构，符号验证在系统层面补充神经策略提升答案可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过验证器引导的RLVR有效增强了模型推理的显式结构和可解释性，同时符号验证机制在系统层面弥补了神经网络的可靠性不足。两者互补实现了透明、可验证的教育问答推理，为构建可信赖的推理系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong reasoning ability, but their explanations can remain inconsistent, weakly grounded, or difficult to verify. We propose a verifier-guided explainable reasoning framework for transparent educational question answering that combines gold-anchored QLoRA, task-aware symbolic routing, and group-relative RLVR. Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct is first adapted with field-weighted QLoRA supervision anchored to authoritative answers. A lightweight router then assigns logic problems to a FOL/Z3 verifier and physics problems to a formula- and unit aware symbolic solver. Verifier feedback is further used to support candidate evaluation, self-revision, and reward construction during RLVR. Candidate responses are evaluated along three complementary dimensions: P1 for answer correctness, P2 for evidence or unit consistency, and P3 for reasoning depth and explainability. At inference, gold-free self-consistency aggregates multiple candidate responses before an optional question-only physics verifier performs conservative system-level correction. On 438 held-out examples, RLVR increases P3 from 50.68% to 72.20%, while hybrid P1 remains approximately stable at 55.94%. Self-consistency improves model only P1 from 48.86% to 50.23%, with symbolic verification providing the remaining hybrid gain. These results indicate that RLVR primarily strengthens explicit reasoning structure, while symbolic verification complements the neural policy by improving answer reliability at the system level.

</details>


### [61] [Self-Supervised Lexical Representation Learning for Fast, Large-Scale Phylogenetic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05262)
*Tim Wientzek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全自监督对比学习框架，直接从IPA音标词表学习词汇表示，无需同源词标注，仅用几分钟在普通GPU上即可推断出3399种语言变体的系统发育树，且与Glottolog参考树的广义四元组距离具有竞争力，同时还能捕捉概念稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算系统发育学在历史语言学中至关重要，但全球规模应用受限于人工同源词标注的劳动密集性和大规模数据集推理的高计算成本。

Method: 提出全自监督对比学习框架，包含双重对比目标：词级损失将语音相似形式组织成连贯空间，语言级辅助损失使词汇空间反映更广泛的语言音系特征。从词表示导出成对语言距离，推断全球系统发育树。

Result: 推断的3399语言变体树与Glottolog参考树的GQD具有竞争力，仅需几分钟在标准笔记本GPU上计算。表示还捕捉历时概念稳定性：距离方差产生的稳定性排名与既定排名显著相关。消融研究证实语言级目标和音系特征向量均改善树拓扑的GQD。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了计算高效、全自动的大规模系统发育推断替代方案，并提供统一表示支持语言和概念层面的下游分析。

Abstract: Computational phylogenetics has become an essential tool in historical linguistics, yet its application at a global scale remains constrained by two factors: the labor-intensive manual annotation of cognacy judgments required for character-based methods and the substantial computational cost of inference on large datasets. This paper introduces a fully self-supervised contrastive learning framework that learns lexical representations directly from raw IPA-transcribed wordlists, without requiring cognacy annotations, alignments, or additional expert input. The model employs a dual contrastive objective: a word-level loss that organizes phonetically similar forms into a coherent space, and an auxiliary language-level loss that encourages the lexical space to reflect broader phonological properties of languages. From the resulting word representations, pairwise language distances are derived and used to infer a global phylogenetic tree of 3,399 language varieties. The inferred tree achieves a generalized quartet distance (GQD) to the Glottolog reference tree competitive with multiple baselines, while requiring only minutes of computation on a standard notebook GPU. Furthermore, the same representations capture diachronic concept stability: variance in pairwise distances across languages yields stability rankings that correlate significantly with established rankings. Ablation studies confirm that both the language-level objective and the use of phonetic feature vectors improved the inferred trees topology with regards to GQD. The framework thus provides a computationally efficient and fully automatic alternative for large-scale phylogenetic inference and offers a unified representation supporting downstream analyses at both the language and concept level.

</details>


### [62] [LexFlip: A Dissociation Diagnostic for Legal Meaning Preservation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05296)
*Gaurab Baral*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出现有评估指标无法区分法律条文的表面形式与法律效力，提出 LexFlip 数据集（373 个极小扰动，保留 93% 词汇但翻转法律效力）及评测框架，实验显示嵌入和 BERTScore 指标对法律效力反转不敏感，双向 NLI 表现较好，且简单长度特征在 FrJudge 上超越所有语义指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估简化法律条文是否保留原意的方法将词汇重叠与法律效力混淆，任何单调的词汇重叠函数都能通过现有检查，无法真正验证法律含义是否保持。

Method: 构建 LexFlip 数据集：对魁北克法规法语进行最小扰动，保留 93% 词汇但反转法律效力；设计评测框架对嵌入指标、BERTScore、双向 NLI 及提示式判断器进行打分；在 FrJudge 基准上对比人类上限。

Result: 七个嵌入和 BERTScore 指标在 identical-to-unrelated 范围内仅占用 0.022–0.039，双向 NLI 占用 0.670；在 FrJudge 上，简单长度特征以 r=0.597 的人类上限超越所有语义指标，且误差最小。

Conclusion: 现有语义相似度指标无法捕捉法律效力的细微变化，需开发对法律语义敏感的评估方法；LexFlip 为该问题提供了关键测试床。

Abstract: Does a simplified legal clause still say what the original said? The checks in current use cannot establish that it does: requiring an identical pair to score highest and an unrelated pair lowest moves lexical overlap and legal force together, so any monotone function of token overlap satisfies both. Our remedy is a dissociation, an item holding surface form fixed while legal force moves. We release LexFlip, 373 minimal perturbations of Quebec statutory French that reverse legal force while preserving 0.93 of the tokens, with a harness scoring metrics, regressors and prompted judges alike. The seven embedding and BERTScore metrics we test spend only 0.022 to 0.039 of their identical-to-unrelated range on such an edit, against 0.670 for bidirectional NLI, the one family the identical-pair check would disqualify. On FrJudge, against a measured human ceiling of r=0.597, a bare length feature outscores every semantic metric and has the lowest margin we measure.

</details>


### [63] [WearableQA: A Benchmark for Health Reasoning over Real-World Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05405)
*Ji Soo Lee,Xilun Chen,Pierce Chuang,Ashish Shenoy,Jason Wei,Dohwan Ko,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Benoit Corda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WearableQA是一个基于真实用户纵向可穿戴设备数据的基准，包含4084道十选一多项选择题，用于评估大语言模型在可穿戴数据上的推理能力。实验显示模型表现差异大（19.6%-72.9%），基准尚未被解决，多数模型准确率低于60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少评估AI系统是否能对真实用户的纵向可穿戴记录进行推理，需要真实、诊断性的基准来评估LLM在现实世界可穿戴数据上的推理能力。

Method: 构建WearableQA基准：从200名真实用户最长500天的每日测量（可穿戴时间序列、血液生物标志物、人口统计学）中生成4084道十选一多项选择题。保留真实的可穿戴分布（含设备噪声和个体间变异性）。设计16种题型，沿两轴：数据与健康推理，单信号与跨信号推理。采用双重基础框架：文献基础的生理学发现 + 统计验证的人群基础生理学模式。评估14个专有和开源LLM。

Result: 模型性能范围19.6%至72.9%（随机基线10%），多数模型准确率低于60%。WearableQA能有效区分模型能力。

Conclusion: WearableQA提供了一个现实且具有诊断性的基准，用于评估LLM在现实世界可穿戴数据上的推理能力，目前仍远未被解决。

Abstract: Recent advances in wearable sensing enable continuous monitoring of physiological and behavioral signals, yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether AI systems can reason over a real user's longitudinal wearable record. We introduce WearableQA, a benchmark comprising 4,084 10-option multiple-choice questions constructed from the wearable time series, blood biomarkers, and demographics of 200 real users, each with up to 500 days of daily measurements. WearableQA preserves authentic wearable distributions that include device noise and inter-individual variability. To evaluate distinct reasoning capabilities, we introduce 16 question types organized along two complementary axes: data versus health reasoning, which distinguishes computation over longitudinal measurements from physiological interpretation; and single- versus cross-signal reasoning, which separates reasoning about individual signals from the integration of multiple signals. To construct reliable questions at scale, we adopt a dual-grounding framework that combines literature-grounded physiological findings with statistically validated population-grounded physiological patterns. This enables the capture of meaningful relationships observed in real-world wearable data. Evaluation of 14 proprietary and open-source LLMs demonstrates that WearableQA effectively differentiates model capabilities, with performance ranging from 19.6% to 72.9% against a 10% chance baseline. Moreover, WearableQA remains far from solved: most models achieve accuracies below 60%. Overall, WearableQA provides a realistic and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over real-world wearable data.

</details>

### [64] [SAGE: Semantic Attribute Graphs for Multi-Entity Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04255)
*Yongjoo Kim,Mincheol Kwon,Seonga Choi,Minseung Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Hyunyoung Lee,Yunsu Choi,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的框架 SAGE，用于解决密集文档图像中的语义稀释问题，并引入了 DEAR 数据集进行评估，实验显示 SAGE 在细粒度实体检索上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集文档图像包含大量细粒度视觉和文本实体，标准视觉语言检索器使用单一向量编码裁剪区域，导致不同实体信号混合，掩盖了细粒度检索所需的证据，这种现象称为语义稀释，会随着实体密度增加而降低实体级检索性能。

Method: 提出 SAGE 框架：从密集文档图像解析语义实体，将其表示为具有多向量嵌入的分层图节点，通过迭代实体级子图匹配检索查询相关证据。同时构建 DEAR 数据集，包含 1,055 个查询-图像对，涵盖四种复杂度递增的问题类型。

Result: 实验表明 SAGE 大幅减轻语义稀释，在 DEAR 数据集上优于补丁级和基于 OCR 的检索基线，在多实体视觉比较查询上达到 Recall@3 0.849 和生成得分 2.746。

Conclusion: SAGE 通过无需训练的分层图表示和迭代子图匹配，有效解决了密集文档图像中的语义稀释问题，为细粒度实体级检索提供了新范式，且在复杂多实体比较任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Dense document images often contain many fine-grained visual and textual entities whose relevance depends on a user query. Standard vision-language retrievers encode cropped regions with a single vector, which can mix distinct entity signals and obscure the evidence needed for fine-grained retrieval. We call this failure mode Semantic Dilution and quantitatively show that it degrades entity-level retrieval as a function of entity density. To mitigate it, we propose SAGE, a training-free framework that parses semantic entities from dense document images, represents them as hierarchical graph nodes with multi-vector embeddings, and retrieves query-relevant evidence through iterative entity-level subgraph matching. We also introduce DEAR, a dataset of 1,055 query--image pairs sourced from product detail pages, where each query requires retrieving and comparing multiple fine-grained entities from visually dense inputs across four question types of increasing complexity. Experiments show that SAGE substantially reduces semantic dilution and outperforms patch-level and OCR-based retrieval baselines on DEAR, achieving a Recall@3 of 0.849 and a generation score of 2.746 on multi-entity visual comparison queries. Our code is available at https://github.com/All4Nothing/SAGE.

</details>


### [65] [MURAL: Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04574)
*Ahmad Mousavi,Majid Alikhani,Yeon-Chang Lee,Roberto Corizzo,Yeganeh Abdollahinejad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MURAL框架，通过自适应边学习解决多模态推荐中的结构僵化问题，通过不确定性感知融合解决语义脆弱性问题，在大规模基准测试上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态图神经网络推荐面临两大瓶颈：结构僵化——依赖静态预计算相似图无法适应偏好演化；语义脆弱性——嘈杂模态信号被无差别融合，扭曲协同信号。

Method: 提出MURAL统一框架：1) 自适应边学习器结合可微检索增强策略与近似最近邻搜索，发现潜在物品间关联（O(NlogN)复杂度）；2) 不确定性感知融合模块建模异构模态的任意不确定性，动态降权不可靠特征；3) 对比教师-学生对齐将模态特定表示锚定到稳定行为信号。

Result: 在TikTok和Amazon大规模基准测试上，MURAL显著超越结构型和生成型SOTA基线，获得更高准确性，同时通过领域特定模态主导性提供可解释性，并在极端数据损坏下展现鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MURAL将多模态推荐从固定结构增强转向动态拓扑发现，通过自适应边学习和不确定性感知融合有效解决了结构僵化和语义脆弱性两大核心问题，为多模态推荐提供了可扩展、鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Graph Neural Networks have become standard for recommendation by augmenting sparse interaction data with content features. Yet current architectures face two bottlenecks: structural rigidity, from a reliance on static precomputed similarity graphs that cannot adapt to evolving preferences; and semantic fragility, where noisy modality signals are indiscriminately fused, distorting the collaborative signal. We propose MURAL (Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning), a unified framework that shifts multimodal recommendation from fixed structural augmentation to dynamic topology discovery. To address structural rigidity, an Adaptive Edge Learner combines a differentiable retrieval-augmented strategy with an approximate nearest neighbor search to discover latent item-item correlations that are both semantically adaptive and computationally scalable (O(NlogN)). To address semantic fragility, an Uncertainty-Aware Fusion module models the aleatoric uncertainty of heterogeneous modalities, dynamically down-weighting unreliable features while prioritizing high-confidence signals as a defense against cross-modal noise. We further employ a contrastive teacher-student alignment that anchors modality-specific representations to stable behavioral signals, ensuring optimization stability without gradient leakage. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks including TikTok and Amazon show that MURAL significantly surpasses both structural and generative state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior accuracy while offering interpretability through domain-specific modality dominance and robustness under extreme data corruption.

</details>


### [66] [Latent-Aligned Reasoning for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04645)
*Jiarui Jin,Anyang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LARK 是一个两阶段潜在推理框架，用于解决多模态视觉语言模型在推荐任务中因多步推理导致的跨模态稀释问题，通过潜在令牌对齐视觉编码器和桥接 MLP 结合对比学习，实现了多个推荐架构上的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态视觉语言模型在跨模态理解上表现优异，但应用于推荐系统时，视觉和文本信号在多步推理过程中逐渐衰减，导致跨模态稀释，严重影响推荐效果。

Method: 提出 LARK 两阶段框架：第一阶段，可学习潜在令牌与多步链式思维推理交织，并与冻结视觉编码器显式对齐，作为视觉检查点保留感知细节；第二阶段，通过桥接 MLP 投影潜在表示并采用项到项对比学习训练，同时将中间特征与第一阶段的 CoT 隐藏状态对齐，锚定最终嵌入到模型自身推理输出。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个工业数据集上实验，LARK 在多种推荐架构上均达到最先进性能，消融实验验证了每个组件的独特贡献。

Conclusion: LARK 通过双阶段潜在推理与互补对齐机制，有效缓解了跨模态稀释，显著提升了 VLM 在推荐任务中的表现，为多模态推荐提供了新的技术路径。}

Abstract: Multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in cross-modal understanding, yet a fundamental challenge persists when applying them to recommendation: as representations propagate through multi-step reasoning, both visual and textual signals progressively attenuate - a phenomenon we term cross-modal dilution. To address this, we propose LARK (Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK), a two-stage latent reasoning framework with complementary alignment mechanisms within a single VLM. In the first stage, learnable latent tokens are interleaved with multi-step chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and explicitly aligned with a frozen vision encoder, serving as visual checkpoints that preserve perceptual details throughout the reasoning chain. In the second stage, the latent representations are projected via a bridge MLP and trained with item-to-item contrastive learning; to prevent the reasoning semantics from fading, intermediate features are aligned with the CoT hidden states from the first stage, anchoring the final embeddings to the model's own reasoning output. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset show that LARK achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple recommendation architectures, with controlled ablations confirming the distinct contribution of each component.

</details>


### [67] [VizIt: A multi-view framework for exploring single-cell, spatial, and genetic data online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04658)
*Chenhang Christopher Zhang,Yanqing Lou,Jie Yuan,Mingming Lu,Jacob Parker,Himanshu Chintalapudi,Zechuan Lin,Clemens R. Scherzer,Yuxuan Hu,Ruifeng Hu,Xianjun Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VizIt 是一个开源框架，用于单细胞和空间转录组、表观基因组及遗传数据的多视角交互式探索，连接基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域和变异中心的视图，并在帕金森病细胞图谱中展示应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学研究需要从互补的生物学视角检验数据，但现有的交互式探索在不同模态和工具间仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的多视角导航框架。

Method: 开发 VizIt 开源框架，整合基因中心、细胞类型中心、条件中心、空间中心、基因组区域中心和变异中心等六种视角，实现跨生物学视角的无缝导航和交互式探索。

Result: 通过帕金森病细胞图谱演示了 VizIt 的功能，构建了一个可定制的交互式多组学资源，支持多模态数据的统一可视化和探索。

Conclusion: VizIt 为多组学数据提供了统一的多视角交互探索框架，解决了跨模态工具碎片化的问题，为单细胞和空间多组学数据的综合分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Multi-omic studies increasingly require data to be examined from complementary biological perspectives, yet interactive exploration remains fragmented across modalities and tools. We present VizIt, an open-source framework for multi-view exploration of single-cell and spatial transcriptomic, epigenomic and genetic data. VizIt connects gene-, cell type-, condition-, spatial-, genomic region- and variant-centered views, enabling seamless navigation across biological perspectives. We demonstrate VizIt through the Parkinson's Cell Atlas, a customizable interactive multi-omic resource.

</details>


### [68] [Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization for Training-Free AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04813)
*Wei Zhou,Tiandeng Wu,Jiandong Ding,Zhufeng Fan,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO)，一种无需训练的方法，用于固定 AI 搜索管道在频繁更新的商品目录上工作。IGPO 将策略与环境事实分离：离线学习“策略准则”以便在运行时根据库存证据行动，在线通过探测库存构建库存画像并将相关准则注入检索与选择提示。自 2026 年 5 月部署于商业智能助手搜索系统，14 天 A/B 测试显示相对 CTR 提升 3.17%，经审计的坏例减少 38.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期部署的 AI 搜索系统面临频繁更新的商品目录，可用商品及其属性无法视为稳定知识编码进固定提示或策略。微调、强化学习和静态提示补丁均不适用：标签稀缺、奖励随库存漂移、模型发布成本高、提示修复快速失效。因此需要一种免训练、能动地适应运行时库存证据的方法。

Method: IGPO 包含离线与在线两阶段。离线：通过随机演播按查询分组，利用混合结果组产生对比信号，并引入库存引导的探索循环，区分“漏检索路径”与“观测库存下无匹配支持”两类情况，从而学习通用的 Policy Guidelines。在线：对每个查询实时探测库存，构建库存画像，并将相关 Policy Guidelines 注入检索提示与选择提示，实现策略与环境事实的分离。

Result: IGPO 自 2026 年 5 月起在商业智能助手 AI 搜索系统上线。完整 IGPO 处理的 14 天在线 A/B 测试显示：相对点击率 (CTR) 提升 3.17%，经人工审计的坏例减少 38.9%。

Conclusion: IGPO 通过将策略与环境事实解耦，利用免训练的离线准则学习与在线库存接地，有效解决了动态商品目录下 AI 搜索的适应性问题，在生产环境中显著提升了点击率并降低了错误案例比例，证明了在固定管道上注入运行时库存感知策略的实用价值。

Abstract: Early in deployment, an AI search system typically operates over a frequently updated product catalog, so the available items and their properties cannot be treated as stable knowledge that can be encoded in fixed prompts or strategies. Fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and static prompt patches fit poorly: labels are scarce, rewards drift with inventory, model releases are costly, and prompt fixes quickly stale. We present Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO), a training-free approach for fixed AI search pipelines. IGPO separates policy from environment facts: it learns Policy Guidelines for acting on runtime inventory evidence rather than memorizing available items. Online, IGPO grounds each query by probing the inventory and constructing an inventory portrait, then injects relevant Policy Guidelines into the retrieval and selection prompts. Offline, stochastic rollouts are grouped by query -- mixed outcome groups directly yield contrastive signal, and an inventory-guided exploration loop distinguishes missed retrieval routes from cases where no matching support is found under the observed inventory evidence. Since May 2026, IGPO has been deployed in a commercial smart-assistant AI search system. A 14-day online A/B test of the complete IGPO treatment shows a 3.17% relative CTR lift and a 38.9% reduction in audited bad cases.

</details>


### [69] [Personalized Task Dependency Graphs for Mitigating Signal Erosion in Multi-Task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04862)
*Fuyuan Liu,Tiandeng Wu,Yaqun Fang,Wei Zhou,Zehao Zhou,Wenping Chen,Qishun Mei,Jiaxin Zhou,Heng Chang,Yi Cao,Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化任务依赖图（PTDG）解决工业推荐中多转化目标优化问题，通过低秩近似动态调整任务依赖强度，结合GCN传播与硬因果掩码，并引入自适应渐进掩码策略，显著提升稀疏转化任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务学习方法在静态转化漏斗中强制统一依赖强度，忽视任务相关性随物品特征的自然变化，导致层级消息传递产生累积信号衰减，严重损害稀疏深层漏斗目标的表现。

Method: 提出PTDG框架：1) 利用低秩近似为每个物品动态重连任务依赖路径强度，同时保留物理因果约束（如点击→购买）；2) 基于GCN的传播机制配合硬因果掩码建立自适应信息捷径；3) 引入自适应渐进掩码（APM）策略，根据任务稀疏度解耦共享参数以稳定优化。

Result: 在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上实验表明，PTDG将稀疏转化任务AUC提升高达1.45%，稠密目标保持相当表现；线上A/B测试显示CVR提升1.2%，eCPM提升1.9%。

Conclusion: PTDG通过个性化任务依赖建模有效缓解信号侵蚀问题，在保持因果约束的前提下实现自适应信息传播，显著改善工业推荐系统中稀疏转化目标的预测性能，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Optimizing multiple conversion objectives is a core challenge in industrial recommendation, often limited by signal erosion in rigid architectures. Existing Multi-Task Learning (MTL) methods typically enforce uniform dependency strengths across a static conversion funnel, overlooking how task correlations naturally vary based on item characteristics. Hierarchical message passing along these fixed chains leads to cumulative signal attenuation, which degrades performance on sparse, deep-funnel objectives. To address this, we propose the Personalized Task Dependency Graphs (PTDG). While respecting necessary physical causal constraints (e.g., Click -> Pay), PTDG dynamically "rewires" the intensity of dependency pathways for each item via low-rank approximation to ensure structural robustness. We implement a GCN-based propagation with hard causal masking to establish adaptive information shortcuts. Additionally, we introduce an Adaptive Progressive Masking (APM) strategy that decouples shared parameters according to task sparsity, helping to stabilize optimization. Experiments on KuaiRand1K and an industrial dataset show that PTDG significantly improves AUC on sparse conversion tasks by up to 1.45%, while maintaining comparable performance on dense objectives. Online A/B testing shows PTDG improves Conversion Rate (CVR) by 1.2% and effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM) by 1.9% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [70] [AtomRec: Evolving Atomic Memory for Agentic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04882)
*Peiyu Hu,Weihai Lu,Siying Gu,Zhuodong Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Yuean Niu,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AtomRec，一种基于演化原子协作记忆的智能体推荐系统。它将用户与物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元，构建语义链接并随交互演化历史字段，检索多跳证据路径以支撑可解释的协作排序。在四个公开基准上，AtomRec 相对现有最强基线平均提升约 8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推荐系统的记忆机制往往将用户和物品信息压缩为粗粒度摘要，仅用标量协作链接连接，难以保留细粒度偏好阶段，也难在兴趣演化过程中检索可解释的证据。

Method: AtomRec 将用户/物品记忆建模为结构化原子单元，跨相关记忆建立语义链接，新交互到来时演化相关历史字段；推荐时检索链接记忆形成多跳证据路径，而非孤立邻居摘要，从而让协作信号支撑有依据的排序。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上，AtomRec 在多项指标上持续超越最先进的智能体与记忆增强基线，平均相对提升约 8.5%。

Conclusion: 原子化、可演化的协作记忆结构结合多跳证据检索，能有效保留细粒度偏好演化并提供可解释的协作推理，显著提升智能体推荐系统的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems use large language models to maintain semantic memory and support evidence-aware recommendation. However, existing memory mechanisms often compress user and item information into coarse summaries and connect them with scalar collaborative links, making it difficult to preserve fine-grained preference stages or retrieve interpretable evidence as user interests evolve. We propose \textsc{AtomRec}, an agentic recommender with evolving atomic collaborative memory. \textsc{AtomRec} represents user and item memories as structured atomic units, builds semantic links across related memories, and evolves related historical fields when new interactions arrive. During recommendation, it retrieves linked memories as multi-hop evidence paths rather than isolated neighbor summaries, allowing collaborative signals to support grounded ranking. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that \textsc{AtomRec} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art agentic and memory-augmented baselines, with around 8.5\% average relative improvement across metrics.

</details>


### [71] [SAM-D2Q: Aligning Multimodal Doc2Query with Search Demand and Conversion for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04961)
*Hui Zhou,Jian Hui Ji,Lei Ma,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SAM-D2Q，一种面向电商搜索的业务对齐多模态文档扩展框架，通过三阶段训练（多模态微调、数据增强、RL偏好对齐）生成伪查询，显著提升检索性能与在线业务指标（GMV +3.38%，支付数 +2.27%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中用户查询与商家标题存在词汇不匹配，传统Doc2Query仅基于文本且未针对业务目标优化，生成的扩展可能语义合理但商业无效，且忽略图像中的关键属性。

Method: SAM-D2Q包含三阶段：(1) 任务适配的多模态监督微调，增强对商品标题、图像及用户查询的视觉-语言理解；(2) 多模态数据增强，提升关键视觉属性感知与扩展覆盖；(3) 基于强化学习的偏好对齐，引导模型生成更符合用户意图和商业价值的伪查询。

Result: 离线实验显示SAM-D2Q在检索性能上大幅优于传统Doc2Query方法；在AliExpress生产搜索系统部署后，GMV提升3.38%，支付数提升2.27%。

Conclusion: SAM-D2Q通过多模态建模与业务对齐有效解决了电商搜索中的词汇不匹配问题，实现了检索效果与商业指标的双重提升，验证了多模态文档扩展在工业级搜索系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: E-commerce search often suffers from vocabulary mismatch between user queries and merchant-authored product titles, since short titles cannot fully cover diverse user expressions or visual product attributes. Although Doc2Query alleviates this issue by generating pseudo-queries for document expansion, traditional methods are text-only and not optimized for e-commerce business objectives. As a result, they may produce semantically plausible but commercially ineffective expansions and miss key attributes present in product images. To this end, we propose E-commerce Search-Aligned Multimodal Doc2Query (SAM-D2Q), a business-aligned multimodal document expansion framework for e-commerce search under Boolean retrieval constraints. SAM-D2Q consists of three stages: (1) task-adapted multimodal supervised fine-tuning to enhance vision-language understanding of product titles, images, and user queries; (2) multimodal data augmentation to improve perception of key visual attributes and expansion coverage; and (3) reinforcement-learning-based preference alignment toward search business objectives, encouraging the model to generate pseudo-queries that better match user intent and commercial value. Offline experiments show that SAM-D2Q substantially improves retrieval performance over traditional Doc2Query methods. Deployed in the AliExpress production search system, SAM-D2Q improves online business metrics, increasing GMV by +3.38% and Pay Count by +2.27%.

</details>


### [72] [Repeated Queries Exhaust an LLM's Brand Recommendations but Not Its Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05059)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究考察重复相同购买提问是否会耗尽语言模型的品牌推荐，发现无检索引擎在第15轮仍大量新增品牌（86-92%单元格），中位推荐集15-31个；检索增强引擎更早收敛（中位8个，64%仍新增），域名累积持续上升。单次运行覆盖五轮品牌集的62-77%，跨引擎中位推荐38品牌，其中15个仅见于单一引擎。开放抽取消除了固定名单带来的虚假平台期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在重复查询下的品牌推荐多样性边界，以及检索机制如何影响推荐集的闭合与领域覆盖，揭示评估方法（固定名单vs开放抽取）对饱和度判断的影响。

Method: 实验设计：50个问题×6引擎×15轮运行，共300问-引擎单元，开放抽取并裁决1,470个组织。对比无检索（5引擎）与有检索（1引擎）引擎。深度单元延伸至24轮。使用精确稀释曲线与Chao2丰度估计量；平行固定名单抽取作为对照。

Result: 无检索引擎第15轮仍在86-92%单元格新增品牌，中位推荐集15-31；检索引擎中位8品牌，64%单元格仍新增，四个深度单元第10轮饱和。域名累积持续上升：深度单元第24轮仍新增，观测到Chao2下界59-84%；检索引擎44%广度单元第15轮仍新增域名。单轮覆盖五轮品牌集62-77%，跨引擎中位推荐38品牌，15个仅单一引擎出现。固定名单抽取产生平坦曲线，开放抽取移除人为平台期。

Conclusion: 无检索语言模型的品牌推荐空间极大且未饱和，检索机制显著加速收敛但仍未完全闭合。评估需采用开放抽取与丰度估计量，避免固定名单导致的虚假饱和结论。跨引擎差异大，单一运行不可靠，需多轮聚合。

Abstract: Whether repeated identical buying questions exhaust a language model's brand recommendations depends on retrieval. Across 300 question-engine cells (50 questions, six engines, 15 runs each, open extraction over 1,470 adjudicated organizations), the five engines answering without web search were still adding never-seen brands at run 15 in 86-92% of cells, with median repertoires of 15-31 organizations; the one retrieval-enabled engine closed its list (median 8 organizations, 64% of cells still adding), matching four earlier deep cells where web-search runs saturated by run ten. Cited-domain accumulation keeps rising at every horizon tested: four deep cells were still adding domains at run 24 with 59-84% of the Chao2 lower-bound estimate observed, and 44% of the retrieval engine's breadth cells were still adding domains at run 15. A single run shows 62-77% of the five-run brand set, and across engines the median question draws 38 organizations, of which a median of 15 appear in exactly one engine. Estimators are exact rarefaction and Chao2 richness; a parallel fixed-roster extraction reproduces flat curves on identical responses, so roster-bounded tracking manufactures plateaus that open extraction removes.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Co-purchase Relation: Evolution of Complementary Recommendations at Allegro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05063)
*Aleksandra Osowska-Kurczab,Klaudia Nazarko,Eliška Kosturová,Lidia Wojciechowska,Michał Bień*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlleCompanion，一个部署在 Allegro.com 的生产级互补商品检索框架，通过数据级启发式过滤、类别约束的双塔架构、Category Adapter 以及多源互补类别映射 ComCat，有效解决大规模共购信号中的噪声问题，实现精准的语义兼容性推荐，月服务 2000 万活跃用户，显著提升有机发现归因 GMV 与赞助位收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互补商品推荐对完整购物篮构建至关重要，但现有模型难以区分「仅被一起购买」与「真正协同工作」的商品，大规模共购行为信号包含大量噪声，导致推荐质量不足。

Method: 1. 数据级启发式过滤降噪；2. 类别约束的双塔架构，引入 Category Adapter 在嵌入空间引导模型，限制候选集在逻辑互补边界内；3. ComCat 多源互补类别映射，融合专家规则、人工反馈、LLM 推理与统计挖掘，作为可维护可控的转译层，将噪声流量提炼为有意义的互补模式。

Result: 实验表明，显式类别级约束与神经网络架构结合能有效过滤共购噪声，推荐结果满足真实用户需求。系统月服务 2000 万活跃用户，有机发现归因 GMV 显著提升，赞助位收入实现大幅增长。

Conclusion: AlleCompanion 通过类别约束双塔架构与 ComCat 映射层，将噪声行为信号转化为精准语义兼容性，构建了可扩展、可控、生产就绪的互补商品推荐系统，验证了显式知识注入神经检索的有效性。

Abstract: When a customer adds a professional camera to their cart, should the system suggest a matching lens, a generic tripod, or another camera body? Complementary Product Recommendation is vital for comprehensive basket building, yet standard models often fail to distinguish between items that are merely bought together and those that truly work together. In this paper, we present AlleCompanion: a production-scale retrieval framework deployed at Allegro.com that transforms noisy behavioural signals into precise semantic compatibility. We mitigate the intrinsic noise in large-scale co-purchase traffic by combining data-level filtering heuristics with a category-constrained Two Tower architecture. Within this framework, the Category Adapter guides the model in the embedding space, constraining candidates within logically complementary boundaries. Since modelling authentic user behaviour at scale is inherently difficult, we introduce ComCat, a multi-source Complementary Categories Mapping. ComCat acts as a translational layer that distils meaningful patterns from noisy traffic into a maintainable and controllable solution, integrating expert rules, human-in-the-loop feedback, LLM-based reasoning, and statistical mining. Our experimental results demonstrate that combining explicit category-level constraints with neural architectures effectively filters out co-purchase noise to surface recommendations that satisfy real-world user needs. Serving over 20 million active users monthly, the framework delivers significant uplifts in attributed GMV for organic discovery and drives substantial revenue growth in sponsored placements.

</details>


### [74] [Embedding Surgery: Localized Updates for Adaptive Ranking Correction in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05110)
*Maddalena Amendola,Antonio Mallia,Raffaele Perego*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出“嵌入手术”，一种轻量级自适应排序修正方法，在查询时对选定文档嵌入进行局部最小化更新，以满足排序约束，显著提升稠密检索性能，且计算开销低、不破坏全局嵌入空间结构，可直接在近似最近邻索引上原地覆盖更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统将文档嵌入离线计算并存储在静态索引中，难以适应用户反馈或不断变化的搜索意图，导致排序效果次优。现有方法多侧重查询侧调整，缺乏对文档侧表示的灵活修正机制。

Method: 将嵌入手术建模为凸优化问题：在查询时刻，依据编辑反馈、用户交互或大语言模型伪标签，对选定文档嵌入施加局部最小化扰动，以满足成对排序约束，同时最小化对原始表示的偏离。更新可直接在近似最近邻索引中原地覆盖，无需重建索引。

Result: 在 TREC Deep Learning、TREC Robust、TREC CAsT 和 MS MARCO 基准上持续提升：在 DL-Hard 编辑反馈设置下 nDCG@10 相对提升高达 +60.64%；对噪声或漂移反馈鲁棒；修正效果可泛化至语义相关查询；与查询自适应方法（如 CoRocchio）互补，组合后进一步增益且更抗噪。

Conclusion: 嵌入手术为稠密检索提供了一种高效、可扩展且即插即用的自适应排序修正范式，通过极低代价的文档嵌入局部更新即可实现显著性能提升，且兼容现有 ANN 索引架构，为实际部署中的动态排序调整提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems are core components of modern search engines, recommendation platforms, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines. They encode documents and queries into dense embeddings, enabling efficient semantic search via vector similarity. However, because document embeddings are computed offline and stored in static indexes, these systems struggle to adapt to user feedback or evolving search intent. To address this limitation, we introduce \emph{embedding surgery}, a lightweight approach for adaptive ranking correction in dense retrieval. The method applies localized, minimal updates to selected document embeddings at query time, guided by editorial feedback, user interactions, or pseudo-labels from large language models. We formulate embedding surgery as a convex optimization problem that enforces ranking constraints while minimizing modifications to the affected document representations. We integrate embedding surgery into standard dense retrieval pipelines and evaluate it on TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT, and MS MARCO benchmarks. Results show consistent improvements (e.g., up to +60.64\% relative improvement in nDCG@10 on DL-Hard under editorial feedback), even under noisy or shifting feedback, with low computational cost and without disrupting the global structure of the embedding space. Extensive experiments show that ranking corrections propagate to semantically related queries and that embedding updates can be applied safely and efficiently to scalable Approximate Nearest Neighbor indexes via simple in-place overwriting, without requiring costly index reconstruction. Finally, embedding surgery complements query adaptation methods such as CoRocchio, yielding additional gains while being more robust to noisy feedback.

</details>

### [75] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EXAONE Finance，一种针对金融时间序列预测的基础模型，采用无注意力架构（因果 1D 卷积 + 分组感知池化 MLP）实现线性计算复杂度，并通过掩码上下文增强处理缺失数据，在大规模金融语料上预训练，在 FinVerse 基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要面向通用领域，依赖自注意力机制导致计算成本随序列长度和变量数二次增长，且假设输入完全观测、预训练语料缺乏金融市场独特动态，难以应用于长序列、多通道、间歇性观测的金融面板数据。

Method: 1) 无注意力架构：用因果 1D 卷积进行时序混合，用分组感知池化 MLP 进行变量混合，实现线性时间复杂度。2) 掩码上下文增强：训练时暴露连续缺失片段，增强对金融数据普遍缺失的鲁棒性。3) 大规模金融语料预训练：覆盖股票、外汇、大宗商品、加密资产、固定收益和宏观指标等多资产类别。

Result: 在 FinVerse 金融预测基准上，EXAONE Finance 在三大评测维度——点预测准确性、横截面资产排序、组合盈利能力——均排名第一，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: EXAONE Finance 通过注意力自由架构、缺失数据增强和多资产预训练，有效解决了现有 TSFM 在金融领域的计算效率、缺失数据鲁棒性和领域适应性问题，为金融时间序列预测提供了强有力的基础模型。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [76] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统性回顾了招聘领域人工智能的发展历程，从双边检索和行为排序发展到神经人岗匹配、大语言模型组件及工具使用型招聘智能体。通过分析40篇代表性文献，识别出三大耦合转变：从相似度到双向适配、从单一模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到证据与生产力对齐的评估。指出了行为标签混淆、数据外部效度受限、评分掩盖管道失效、隐私评估缺失等持久性差距，提出了从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射，以及构建互惠、证据基础、时序可控、选择性和可审计系统的议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘领域的AI已从简单的档案配对和排名列表转向多阶段工作流，涉及证据检索、候选人比较及决策支持或执行。现有研究缺乏对这一演进的系统性综合，特别是缺少对评估证据层级、持久性差距及可辩护主张映射的结构化分析，需要建立评估进展的框架。

Method: 采用目的性搜索和编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日，定向更新至2026年9月2日），整理40篇代表性文献及工业法律来源。分析三大耦合转变，区分文档理解、检索、排序、评估、面试、寻源、人工交接等环节中的字段级、对级、列表级、案例级、轨迹级、结果级六类证据。识别编码集合中的持久性差距，提出分阶段证据到主张映射及系统建设议程。

Result: 识别出三大转变：相似度→双向适配、单一模型→复合工作流、离线预测→证据与生产力对齐评估。揭示四大持久性差距：行为标签混淆曝光/偏好/资质；私有/合成数据限制外部效度；最终输出分数掩盖管道失效；隐私未直接评估且无研究联合评估效用、公平性、隐私、安全。提出分阶段证据-主张映射框架，及互惠、证据基础、时序可控、选择性、可审计的系统建设议程。

Conclusion: 招聘AI进展应以工作流是否检索正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争辩决策、在显性成本风险约束下改善结果为判断标准。需超越单一指标评估，建立多维度、证据基础、可审计的评价体系，推动系统向互惠适配、时间可控、选择性介入方向发展。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>

*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Harbor Adapters，一个统一的智能体基准评估基础设施，将 80 多个基准适配至任意智能体，并在 54 个基准上对 8 个模型进行大规模评测，同时发布了精选的高难度任务集 Harbor-Index（82 个任务，29 个基准），最强配置通过率仅 28.0%，所有工件开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准数量快速增长，但评测困难：需要复杂环境与智能体集成，缺乏统一接口，难以横向对比不同模型在多样任务上的能力与失效模式。

Method: 1) 开发基准适配器，将 80+ 基准移植为统一接口，通过代码审查与平价实验验证；2) 在 54 个基准上用 Terminus-2 与 3 种原生 harness 评测 8 个跨层级模型；3) 通过难度筛选、AI 与人工审计、审计-修复循环，从适配套件中提炼出 82 个高质量高难度任务组成 Harbor-Index。

Result: 完成大规模横向评测，揭示更全面的智能体能力图谱与失效模式；Harbor-Index 保持挑战性与广度且运行成本可控，所有模型-harness 配置通过率均不超过 30%，最强配置（GPT-5.5 + Codex）为 28.0%。

Conclusion: Harbor Adapters 与 Harbor-Index 为智能体评测提供了可复现、可扩展、高质量的开源基础设施，推动更可靠、更全面的语言模型智能体评估。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [78] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用机器学习算法（KNN、随机森林、SVM）对电力系统N-k故障场景进行安全等级分类（安全、中度、严重），采用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流计算获取数据，以OPI为安全指标，结合SMOTE解决类别不平衡和PCA降维预处理，在IEEE-14和IEEE-30节点系统上验证。随机森林表现最佳（F1达0.97和0.86），PCA对整体性能贡献大于SMOTE，SMOTE虽提升召回率但可能引入假阳性。研究表明机器学习是传统故障分析的可扩展替代方案，可提升实时安全评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统安全对稳定性和可靠性至关重要，尤其在扰动发生时。有效的故障分类能支持主动决策，防止大规模故障。传统故障分析计算量大、难以实时应用，因此需要可扩展的机器学习方法实现快速、准确的安全等级分类。

Method: 1. 数据生成：采用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流法计算IEEE-14和IEEE-30节点系统在N-k（k=1,2,3）故障场景下的数据，以整体性能指数（OPI）量化安全等级（安全、中度、严重）。2. 预处理：归一化、SMOTE过采样解决类别不平衡、PCA降维，及四种组合配置。3. 模型训练与评估：训练KNN、随机森林、SVM，使用精确率、召回率、F1分数评估，重点关注严重故障类别。

Result: 随机森林在两系统上F1分数最高（IEEE-30: 0.97，IEEE-14: 0.86）。SVM在PCA降维后性能显著提升。KNN最适合SMOTE+PCA组合。PCA对整体模型性能贡献大于SMOTE。SMOTE虽提高严重类别召回率，但可能引入假阳性，是准确率的折中。四种预处理配置中，SMOTE+PCA组合在多数情况下表现最优。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法（特别是随机森林）结合适当预处理（PCA为主、SMOTE为辅）能高效、准确地实现电力系统故障安全等级分类，为实时安全评估提供了可扩展的强大替代方案，优于传统计算密集型故障分析。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [79] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个搜索智能体Iris-mini(35B)和Iris-pro(397B)，通过从网页超链接结构反向构建多跳推理任务，结合SFT-RL攀爬训练范式，在BrowseComp等基准上达到开源搜索智能体同参数量下的最强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体训练数据往往依赖字符串匹配线索，缺乏真实的多跳推理挑战；且推理时上下文管理对性能影响大于系统间差异，需要系统性的数据构建、训练和评估方法来提升搜索智能体的真实推理能力。

Method: 1) 从种子页面及其出链构建实体图，反向生成多跳推理链；2) 将非答案实体重写为描述性引用，消除字符串匹配捷径；3) 仅保留闭卷失败但有证据可解的问题；4) 轨迹级和轮次级过滤后SFT；5) 基于实时搜索的RL优化，奖励判断器和观察摘要器在训练集群内服务；6) 过长轨迹在请求级中断并从已提交前缀恢复；7) SFT-RL攀爬：交替两阶段，将每轮RL最难解决和最高效轨迹回传下一轮SFT；8) 评估时固定工具集、上下文限制和评判器，对比启用/禁用上下文管理的结果。

Result: 启用上下文管理时，Iris-mini在BrowseComp/BrowseComp-ZH/DeepSearchQA/HLE上得分为82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3；Iris-pro得分为88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4。两模型均为其参数区间内开源搜索智能体最强整体表现。单ReAct智能体，无子智能体、无测试时验证。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的数据反向构建、SFT-RL攀爬训练范式和严格的评估协议，本文训练出的两个规模搜索智能体在多个基准上达到SOTA开源水平，并计划开源模型权重及完整数据构建、训练和评估配方。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [80] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种测试时方法，通过移除解释中未提及的概念并重新查询模型，来解决大语言模型解释中的不完整性问题，提高解释的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多用于重要决策，其解释对审计模型行为至关重要，但解释往往不忠实，存在不完整性（遗漏影响答案的因素）和不合理性（引用未影响答案的因素）两个维度。现有方法主要针对不合理性，且训练时方法需要模型权限和大量计算资源。

Method: 在测试时，从输入中移除模型解释中未提及的概念，然后在缩减后的输入上重新查询模型。这消除了未提及的影响，同时保留了提及概念的影响。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族和两个独立忠实性指标上，该方法优于标准提示和鼓励忠实性的提示。

Conclusion: 该方法模型无关，可在推理时应用而无需修改模型参数，为减少隐性影响和提高LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性提供了灵活机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [81] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型能力提升可能导致系统层面表现恶化，因为共享训练和架构使高能力模型行为高度相关，产生不可分散的风险底线；在金融市场仿真中验证了这一“能力悖论”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在真实系统（如金融市场、内容审核、招聘）大规模部署时，个体能力提升对系统层面结果的影响，揭示共享训练导致的行为相关性如何产生不可分散风险。

Method: 建立理论框架分析相关性如何形成不可分散风险底线，并通过基于智能体的金融市场仿真实验，使用不同通用能力的LLM交易者进行测试。

Result: 1) 前沿LLM表现出显著相关行为，随能力增强而增加；2) 共享推理准确时，增加智能体参与降低市场风险；3) 共享错误信息环境时，相关行为成为负债。

Conclusion: 存在“能力悖论”：提升个体模型能力不一定改善系统层面结果；该动态是否在其他领域出现仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [82] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，解决企业AI部署因缺乏结构化决策底层而失败的问题。CLM包含五大能力平面与四大架构支柱：神经符号网格、技能图谱、活体数字孪生、深度安全层，采用Spec-as-Code范式实现落地意图到可执行制品的桥接。贡献包括：将CLM定义为独立研究对象、引入技能图谱实现构造性组合可解释性、提出“智慧监听器效应”描述隐性知识基础随使用增值、并在巴西JCI认证三级医院验证LGPD合规下的三个成熟度阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败根源不在于模型性能，而在于组织缺乏编码决策、谈判、执行逻辑的结构化底层。通用LLM不具备企业特定本体先验；RAG脆弱且无法通向可执行动作；静态剧本虽编码逻辑但无法推理与自适应。因此需要从一开始就协同设计隐性知识捕获、本体落地、主权部署、可审计执行的架构。

Method: 构建企业语言模型（CLM）框架，包含五大能力平面与四大架构支柱：1) 神经符号网格：耦合生成模型与知识图谱；2) 技能图谱：将可复用战术、人设、异议、目标类型化并组合；3) 活体数字孪生：将职能领域建模为推理代理；4) 深度安全层：强制主权、可追溯性、人工监督。采用Spec-as-Code范式连接落地意图与可执行制品。

Result: 四项核心贡献：① CLM作为独立研究对象被形式化定义；② 技能图谱实现构造性组合可解释性；③ 提出“智慧监听器效应”，阐述隐性知识型基础随使用复合增值，关联动态能力与组织学习；④ 在巴西JCI认证三级医院实证部署，在LGPD合规框架下实例化六个成熟度阶段中的三个。

Conclusion: CLM代表一类以基础设施为中心的企业AI架构，通过本体落地、技能组合、数字孪生代理、安全治理的协同设计，解决企业知识从隐性到显性、从静态到动态、从碎片到可执行的系统性难题，为组织级AI提供可演进、可审计、主权可控的范式。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [83] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench 是首个为智能体避免副作用（伤害活体生物）定价的基准，采用农场模拟环境，让 LLM 子智能体驾驶拖拉机收割玉米，途中遇到动物阻挡需决定是否付费绕行，以此量化模型为避免伤害愿支付的代价，而非仅测试其言辞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准虽评估智能体达成目标途中造成的副作用，但未对避免副作用定价，也未将副作用明确为活体生物。HarvestBench 旨在填补这一空白，通过显性成本权衡，测量模型在真实决策中为避免伤害生物愿支付多少代价，并考察提示词、价格敏感度、生物类型等因素的影响。

Method: 构建基于强化学习的网格世界农场模拟，两台拖拉机协作收割玉米，田间随机放置动物、干草捆、石头。智能体无记忆，目标中不提伤害。当动物阻挡路径时，自动驾驶暂停并询问模型：直行（零燃料成本）或绕行（支付标价燃料）。以石头（撞击损坏拖拉机，各模型撞击率<1%）和干草捆（无害、非生命）为对照。同时设置偷摘邻地作物任务测试道德判断。跑九个模型，共 7,201 次定价决策，其中 3,951 涉及动物。记录游戏日志事件计数，无需 LLM 评分器，完全可复现。

Result: 击杀率跨度 0.4%–98.8%，Terra 和 Sol 最仁慈，GPT-4o-mini 最残忍，与能力排名无关。六模型中有四个对价格在 5% 水平显著敏感，弹性系数 0.09–1.69。所有九模型在默认地图上撞击野生动物频率高于养殖动物，且在所有地图几何结构下均保持该趋势。提示词影响最大：道德提示下五个推理模型击杀率<6%，移除提示后六模型击杀率均>84%。

Conclusion: HarvestBench 通过可复现的日志计数方式，直接度量模型为避免伤害生物愿支付的实际代价，揭示了模型在隐性道德困境中的真实行为，证明提示词对道德行为有决定性影响，且价格敏感度因模型而异，为 AI 安全与对齐提供了新的量化评估工具。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [84] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PerfReasoning基准，用于评估大语言模型在性能建模任务中的两种角色：直接性能推理器和分析性性能模型代码生成器。实验显示闭源模型在推理问答上超90%，开源模型达82.4%，但模型构建（代码生成）难度大得多：仅GPT-5.6 Sol通过率超80%，其余配置均低于15%且波动大。针对性强化学习可将4B模型推理准确率提升15.7分，而无反馈的多轮自我修正提示不可靠。该基准揭示了似是而非的架构推理与可靠的性能模型构建之间的巨大鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模是硬件设计和软件优化的核心，但构建这些模型需要对计算、数据复用、存储和数据移动进行结构化推理。现有缺乏系统性基准来评估LLMs在此类任务上的能力，尤其是区分“推理问答”与“代码生成/模型构建”两种不同层面的能力。

Method: 构建PerfReasoning基准，包含给定负载、架构和映射规范下的映射比较、片外流量预测和缓冲区需求预测任务。评估闭源/开源LLMs作为直接推理器和代码生成器的表现；引入任务特定强化学习（RL）微调小模型；对比无反馈多轮自我修正提示策略的有效性。

Result: 推理问答：最强闭源模型>90%，最佳开源模型82.4%。模型构建（代码生成）：GPT-5.6 Sol通过率>80%，其余配置平均<15%且方差大。RL微调使4B模型映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点。无反馈多轮自我修正提示效果不稳定。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLMs在“看似合理的架构推理”与“可靠的性能模型构建”之间存在显著能力鸿沟。基准将公开发布，以支持可复现评估并追踪未来进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [85] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出循环状态写回机制作为低精度循环神经网络量化的关键因素，通过荧光寿命成像任务实验表明：固定模型下将连续状态传播替换为4位确定性存储会导致参数估计误差剧增（τ1约70倍，τ2约300倍），主要源于微小更新低于写入阈值被抑制；引入误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆可在不重训练下恢复精度；精度扫描显示提高状态精度反而可能恶化固定循环解，而匹配训练可学习兼容状态接口；在独立训练的LSTM中同样观测到写回失效与误差反馈修复，且细胞状态比隐藏状态更敏感。确立了状态存储接口作为量化循环推理的核心设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化广泛用于降低神经网络推理的计算和内存需求，但在循环网络中，量化状态被存储并在下一时间步返回，状态存储规则（即写回机制）会改变后续计算。现有研究缺乏对循环状态写回机制的系统分析及其对低精度循环动力学影响的隔离研究。

Method: 在紧凑GRU编码器-解码器上针对荧光寿命成像任务（从高噪声时间分辨荧光信号估计短寿命分量τ1和长寿命分量τ2）进行实验：1) 固定预训练模型，将连续状态传播替换为确定性4位状态存储，隔离写回效应；2) 分析失败模式（微小更新低于写入阈值被抑制）；3) 提出误差反馈、残差记忆、方向记忆三种无需重训练的补偿机制；4) 进行状态精度扫描与匹配训练实验；5) 在独立训练的LSTM上复现写回失效与误差反馈修复，并进行状态特定干预对比细胞状态与隐藏状态的敏感性。

Result: 1) 4位确定性状态存储使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍；2) 失败源于重复微小更新低于写入阈值，导致存储状态近似固定而网络持续提议变化；3) 误差反馈、残差记忆、方向记忆可跨时间传递被抑制更新信息，在不重训练下恢复精度；4) 精度扫描显示提高状态精度可能恶化固定循环解，匹配训练可学习兼容状态接口；5) LSTM实验复现写回失效与误差反馈修复，细胞状态比隐藏状态对粗粒度写回更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态写回是决定低精度循环动力学的关键因素，状态存储接口是量化循环推理的核心设计考量。通过显式建模写回机制并引入跨时间信息传递机制，可在不重训练或通过匹配训练的方式有效缓解量化带来的精度退化。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned.
