To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [86] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ResLearn-XR 是一种残差学习框架，用于预测 XR 网络流量和估计 QoE 风险，采用两阶段时序学习结构，在流量预测和 QoE 风险估计上显著降低 SMAPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR 流量具有突发性和非平稳性，现有单阶段模型难以适应；加密流量分析缺乏帧时序感知特征；缺乏配对的 XR 流量与用户 QoE 标签数据集。

Method: 提出 ResLearn-XR，包含基础序列预测模型和任务特定残差学习组件的两阶段时序学习结构；残差学习在值空间进行连续流量预测，在 logit 空间进行概率 QoE 风险估计；引入数据描述符算法 (DDA) 从包级应用层可观测量构建帧时序感知描述符；构建 XR Traffic-QoE 数据集。

Result: 在帧计数、帧大小、帧间到达时间预测上 SMAPE 降低高达 17.84%；QoE 风险估计 SMAPE 降低高达 87.8%，优于单阶段基线。

Conclusion: ResLearn-XR 通过残差学习和 DDA 有效解决了 XR 流量预测和加密 QoE 风险估计的挑战，为 XR 网络质量管理提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [87] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将间接提示词注入建模为测试时搜索问题，提出具备环境侦察、策略推理和自适应评估能力的智能体攻击者，实验表明增加攻击者测试时计算量显著提升漏洞发现与利用率，且显式策略管理对维持大预算下的增益至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估多将攻击成功率视为受害者模型的固有属性，忽略了攻击者在测试时通过自适应搜索攻击面（由环境、用户任务、注入任务共同诱导）来提升攻击效果的能力，亟需从攻击者搜索过程和计算预算角度重新审视工具使用型智能体的安全风险。

Method: 构建一个智能体攻击者及专用搜索框架：1) 环境侦察以映射攻击面；2) 对攻击策略进行结构化推理；3) 利用受害者智能体反馈进行自适应评估；将间接提示词注入形式化为在任务相关攻击面上的测试时搜索过程。

Result: 在异构任务上，增加攻击者测试时计算量持续提升漏洞发现与利用成功率；消融实验证实显式策略管理能避免冗余搜索，在大预算下维持性能增益；攻击成功率强依赖于攻击者的搜索过程与计算预算，而非仅取决于受害者模型本身。

Conclusion: 智能体安全评估必须同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程和计算预算，不能将攻击成功视为与预算无关的受害者属性。攻击者对系统攻击面的自适应搜索是工具使用型智能体一个重要且未被充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [88] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync提出了一种多模态融合架构，将心率变异性、脑电、活动记录和语音等可穿戴设备测量结合为连续复合数字生物标志物BioSync Index (BSI)。采用多头自注意力机制与线性分支相结合的宽深架构，在两个合成队列中优于简单拼接基线，且对模态缺失表现出更强鲁棒性。BSI与潜在严重程度高度相关，但需真实队列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴和移动设备提供的心脏、神经、行为和语音测量仅能提供部分、噪声敏感的生理状态视图。需要将这些异构模态融合为统一的连续复合数字生物标志物，基于潜变量测量理论和联合观测可能包含单模态无法获取信息的假设。

Method: BioSync采用多头自注意力处理模态token，并添加线性分支（假设类包含标准特征拼接），形成宽深架构。在两个文献告知的合成队列评估：四模态认知衰退队列（HRV、EEG、活动记录、语音）和基于公开AI-READI可穿戴模式的代谢-自主神经队列。对比基线包括拼接融合、纯注意力消融、单模态、早融合、晚融合设计，并采用匹配模态Dropout训练评估鲁棒性。

Result: 认知队列中BioSync AUC 0.928 vs 拼接0.926；代谢队列准确率/F1 0.764/0.766 vs 0.756/0.758。BSI与潜在严重程度相关性分别为r=0.91和r=0.68。纯注意力消融AUC 0.911，证实宽深架构贡献。模态Dropout鲁棒性测试中，BioSync在六个认知队列损坏率中的五个和最高代谢队列损坏率下优于拼接。认知队列AUC超越五个已发表数字生物标志物参考值。六项预设标准比较揭示了计算特性。

Conclusion: BioSync通过注意力机制与线性分支结合，有效整合多模态生理信号生成鲁棒的复合数字生物标志物，在合成数据上展现出优于拼接基线的性能和抗缺失模态能力。但需在真实临床队列上进一步验证其泛化性和临床效用。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [89] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多 harness 强化学习中分组规则（Within vs Cross）对代理性能的影响。实验发现评估 harness 是主导变量（解决率从 2.14% 提升至 9.27%），而分组规则无显著差异。Cross-harness 信用分配仅带来配置适应，不增强可迁移能力。建议多 harness RL 报告须说明分组边界并在未见 harness 上测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 Agent RL 越来越多地通过完整执行 harness 运行，多 harness 配方混合两个选择：让策略接触多个 harness，以及在一个相对优势组内比较它们的奖励。本文旨在隔离第二个选择（分组规则），在仓库级编码任务中系统对比 Within（每个 task-harness 对一组）和 Cross（同一 task 内汇聚 harness）两种 GRPO 分组规则的效果。

Method: 从 Qwen3-8B 监督热启动出发，重放来自 Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code、SWE-agent 四个 harness 的冻结 task-harness 记录，使用相同更新步数，分别应用 Within 和 Cross 两种 GRPO 分组规则。在封闭的 SWE-bench Verified oracle 上评估四个源 harness 和一个未参与训练的最小举出 harness，共进行 24,000 次密封评估。同时训练三个随机种子，并重新采集半量训练数据进行 on-policy 实验。

Result: 评估 harness 移动平均解决率 4.3 倍（2.14%→9.27%），训练配方仅移动 1.16 倍。分组规则差异不显著：Cross 减 Within 为 +0.25 pp (95% CI [-0.48, +1.02])，三种子汇总 +0.16 pp (CI [-0.41, +0.72])，单规则内部种子方差（0.42-0.45 pp）超过规则间差异。两规则在相同源 harness 上获得最大增益。Cross 的优势分布携带 harness 信息（分类器比乱序基线高 +4.48 pp），Within 则无。两规则在举出 harness 上达到相同分数和动作分布。On-policy 重采半量数据不改变结论。

Conclusion: Cross-harness 信用分配仅产生配置适应，不带来比 Within 更强的可迁移能力。多 harness RL 研究应明确报告分组边界，并在未见 harness 上进行测试以评估真正的泛化能力。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>


### [90] [MaxKernel: Agentic Kernel Generation for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04523)
*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MaxKernel，一个用于TPU内核开发的多智能体系统，实现了三种不同的开发范式：人工介入（HITL）、完全自动化和基于图的自主搜索，在JaxBench基准测试和真实工作负载上均达到专家手动调优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器设计和编写高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件专业知识，是一项复杂任务。利用大语言模型（LLM）结合实时编译器反馈，构建智能体系统进行内核生成，能够降低专业门槛并提高开发效率。

Method: MaxKernel是一个多智能体系统，包含三种开发范式：(1) 人工介入（HITL）智能体，支持协作式、分步骤设计；(2) 自主智能体，执行完全自动化的指标/追踪驱动优化循环；(3) 基于图的自主搜索，将自主智能体扩展到设计空间的全局探索。所有范式共享专用子智能体池，处理规划、实现、自我调试、测试和硬件分析。

Result: 在包含50个多样化TPU内核任务的JaxBench基准测试以及最先进开源模型的复杂真实工作负载上评估，MaxKernel持续生成高度优化的实现，性能匹配专家手动调优基线，并在基准测试中实现显著性能提升。

Conclusion: MaxKernel通过多智能体协作和多种开发范式，成功解决了TPU内核开发的复杂性问题，实现了与专家相当的自动化内核优化能力，代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling. We evaluate MaxKernel on JaxBench, a comprehensive suite of 50 diverse kernel tasks for TPUs, alongside complex, real-world workloads from state-of-the-art open-source models. We demonstrate that MaxKernel consistently generates highly optimized implementations, matching expert hand-tuned baselines and delivering significant performance across the benchmark. Our agent is open-sourced and available https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxKernel.

</details>


### [91] [Towards a universal language of concepts: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04528)
*Aishni Parab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出程序作为概念的通用表示候选者，回顾了使用程序作为概念表示的计算模型，并评估其对构建通用表示语言的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能从稀疏数据中学习和泛化新概念，归因于其使用丰富的结构化格式表达知识；论文旨在探索程序是否能作为概念的通用表示语言，以实现类人般的概念学习能力。

Method: 通过文献综述的方式，系统梳理并评估现有采用程序作为概念表示的计算概念学习模型，分析其表达能力、学习效率及泛化性能，并探讨其作为通用表示语言的可行性与局限。

Result: 现有基于程序的概念学习模型在结构化表示、组合泛化和小样本学习方面表现出色，验证了程序作为通用表示的潜力；但跨域迁移、规模化学习及高效推理等挑战仍待解决。

Conclusion: 程序是概念通用表示的有力候选者，未来研究需重点解决程序合成的可扩展性、神经符号融合机制以及认知可解释性等问题，以构建真正通用的概念表示语言。

Abstract: Humans can learn and generalize novel concepts from sparse data because they express knowledge in rich structural formats. In this paper, we propose that programs are a strong candidate for universal representation of concepts. We review computational models of concept learning that use programs as their concept representation and evaluate their contribution toward a universal representational language.

</details>


### [92] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APT-RAG 是一个自适应规划与拓扑感知证据收集的 RAG 框架，针对证据密集型多跳问答中的结构刚性和拓扑无感证据收集问题，通过动态扩展推理结构、兄弟节点证据复用、直接检索及子节点证据聚合，以及证据引导的批量答案生成，在基准测试中超越现有结构化 RAG 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于树或图的结构化 RAG 方法在证据密集型问答中面临两大局限：结构刚性限制了自适应推理扩展，拓扑无感证据收集阻碍了跨推理节点的有效证据整合，导致难以综合分散在数十甚至数百文档中的信息。

Method: 提出 APT-RAG 框架，包含三大核心组件：1) 自适应规划：依据问题依赖与证据需求动态扩展推理结构；2) 拓扑感知证据收集：通过兄弟证据复用、直接检索、子节点证据聚合提升证据覆盖；3) 证据引导的批量答案生成：降低证据密集型问答中的生成开销。

Result: 在证据密集型问答基准上，APT-RAG 优于现有结构化 RAG 方法，验证了其在处理大规模证据综合任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: APT-RAG 通过自适应规划与拓扑感知证据收集机制，成功解决了结构刚性与拓扑无感证据收集的关键瓶颈，为证据密集型多跳问答提供了更灵活、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [93] [Data-Driven Discovery of Composition-Dependent Constitutive Models for Hyperelasticity and Viscoelasticity of Digital Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04541)
*Josué García-Ávila,Beijun Shen,Manuel K. Rausch,Mary C. Boyce,Adrián Buganza-Tepole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，推广了Bergström-Boyce模型，利用神经常微分方程(NODEs)和受约束人工神经网络，在保持热力学一致性的前提下捕获多材料3D打印数字材料跨成分和加载速率的速率依赖刚度和滞回行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典有限应变粘弹性模型采用闭合形式的应变能函数描述平衡和非平衡应力及内变量演化，当单一本构模型需要跨材料和加载速率推广时，其灵活性受限。需要一种保留经典模型结构但能从数据中学习的数据驱动方法。

Method: 提出数据驱动多材料本构建模框架，推广Bergström-Boyce公式。保留经典结构：乘法运动学、基于不变量的应变能函数、沿归一化非平衡偏应力方向的标量耗散演化律。平衡分支：数据驱动发现框架要么直接预测闭合形式模型参数作为成分函数，要么利用神经常微分方程(NODEs)自动构建多凸应变能函数。非平衡分支动力学：要么跨成分直接辨识闭合形式参数，要么使用适当受约束的人工神经网络。利用多成分、多速率单轴压缩数据进行训练和验证。

Result: 该公式成功捕获了跨不同成分的速率依赖刚度和滞回行为，同时保持了热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架为多材料3D打印数字材料提供了一个灵活、热力学一致的本构模型，能够跨成分和加载速率进行推广，结合了经典力学结构与数据驱动发现的优势。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [94] [From Answers to Interpretations: Rethinking Ambiguity-Induced Aleatoric Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04543)
*Omer Nahum,Niv Nayman,Jonathan Fhima,Alon Zolfi,Jeremy Levy,Shai Mazor,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需生成答案即可估计输入歧义导致的偶然不确定性的方法，直接从合理解释空间计算歧义度，在三个基准上显著提升AUROC、大幅降低计算成本，并减少与认知不确定性的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性分解方法通过生成多个澄清、查询模型答案并比较答案来估计偶然不确定性，但答案往往冗余、增加成本，且可能通过认知泄漏误导估计。作者认为识别歧义不需要答案，直接从解释空间估计更有效。

Method: 提出仅基于澄清的方法：生成输入的多个合理澄清/解释，直接在解释空间中计算歧义度（如澄清间的分歧或熵），完全跳过对澄清输入的答案生成步骤。

Result: 在三个歧义检测基准上，该方法AUROC提升至63.34（基线60.85），输出token成本降低4-26倍，API调用减少2.2-3.5倍，估计值与认知不确定性的相关性显著降低。

Conclusion: 歧义引起的偶然不确定性应从解释空间而非响应空间估计；无需生成答案即可更准确、高效地量化输入歧义，为可靠LLM部署提供更实用的不确定性分解工具。

Abstract: A key challenge in reliable LLM deployment is recognizing when uncertainty reflects irreducible variability in the task rather than limitations in the model's knowledge. In language tasks, a central source of such aleatoric uncertainty is input ambiguity or underspecification, where multiple interpretations remain plausible. Existing decomposition methods estimate aleatoric uncertainty by generating multiple clarifications of the input, querying the model for an answer under each clarification, and comparing the resulting answers. We argue that answers are not necessary for identifying ambiguity: they are often redundant, add avoidable cost, and can mislead through epistemic leakage. We support this claim theoretically, and propose a clarification-only approach that estimates this ambiguity-induced component directly from the space of plausible interpretations, without answers to the clarified inputs. Using ambiguity detection as an operational evaluation across three benchmarks, this direct approach improves AUROC (63.34 vs. 60.85), reduces computational cost by 4-26x in output tokens and 2.2-3.5x in API calls, and yields estimates with substantially lower correlation with epistemic uncertainty. Overall, our results suggest that ambiguity-induced aleatoric uncertainty is better estimated from the interpretation space than from the response space.

</details>


### [95] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究模型升级时记忆迁移的可靠性，对比四种记忆存储方式：原始长文本(LC-RAW)、分块检索(RAG)、压缩笔记(NOTES)、固定模式知识图谱(KG-fixed)。实验显示KG-fixed迁移最稳健(准确率变化+0.0004±0.0020)，NOTES存在严重模型耦合(迁移方向导致+9.91或-13.28个百分点不对称变化)，RAG部分嵌入迁移仅获4.96分提升而完全重嵌入获11.90分。诊断显示NOTES 80%损失源于构建时信息丢失，RAG 81%损失源于检索失败。仅靠存储修复NOTES无法达90%恢复目标，保留原始历史可在48例中34例成功恢复。结论强调需进行方向特定迁移测试、嵌入空间严格隔离、保留源历史以支持记忆修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常态但记忆迁移并非易事：新模型可能不同解读旧笔记、混合嵌入版本破坏检索、缺乏原始证据导致修复失败。现有研究缺乏对不同记忆存储结构在模型迁移中表现的系统对比与量化分析。

Method: 构建48个合成历史并随机化答案代码，采用精确评分，使用两个开源小模型(<10B参数)。对比四种记忆范式：LC-RAW(原文长文本)、RAG(分块检索增强生成)、NOTES(模型压缩为自然语言笔记)、KG-fixed(固定模式知识图谱)。测量写入模型交换后的准确率变化，分析部分嵌入迁移效果，进行诊断分解归因，评估仅存储修复与保留原始历史修复的效果。

Result: 1) KG-fixed迁移极稳健，准确率变化仅+0.0004±0.0020；NOTES模型耦合强，迁移方向导致准确率不对称变化+9.91或-13.28百分点。2) RAG混合50/50嵌入索引仅获4.96分提升，完全重嵌入获11.90分。3) 诊断分解：NOTES 80%(0.467±0.014)准确率损失源于构建时信息丢失；RAG 81%(0.364±0.012)损失源于检索失败。4) 仅存储修复NOTES在所有48例中均未达90%恢复目标；保留原始历史可在一个测试方向上48例中34例成功恢复。

Conclusion: 固定模式结构(KG)能可靠跨模型迁移，压缩自然语言笔记(NOTES)存在严重模型耦合需方向特定测试，RAG需严格嵌入空间隔离避免混合索引，记忆修复必须保留原始源历史证据。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [96] [IPGeoAI: Transformer-Based Geolocation with LLM Semantic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04559)
*Avinash Kadimisetty,Andy Jinqing Yu,Philip Favaloro,Wenlong Liu,Xiaolu Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IPGeoAI，一种基于Transformer Encoder的深度学习模型，将IP地理定位重构为序列建模任务，结合LLM零样本特征提取AS语义信息，通过跨注意力融合，显著提升城市级定位精度与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式和数据库驱动方法难以应对现代网络基础设施复杂的非线性分配模式，特别是爆炸性增长的IPv6地址空间和瞬态移动网络，导致城市级IP地理定位准确性不足。

Method: 采用Transformer Encoder捕捉IP子网结构的层级依赖；提出零样本LLM特征提取流水线，将非结构化AS描述转换为结构化领域元数据（如大学/ISP、全球/本地）；通过多头交叉注意力模块融合语义信号与网络拓扑；采用分层推理策略细化粗粒度国家信号。

Result: 在包含20万城市的专有数据集上，城市级准确率比领先外部供应商提升6%，覆盖率达100%；大规模在线生产测试中，一级下游用例指标显著提升+0.35%。

Conclusion: IPGeoAI通过深度序列建模与语义增强，有效解决了IP地理定位中的歧义与覆盖难题，实现了城市级粒度的显著性能提升，并已在生产环境验证了实际业务价值。

Abstract: Accurate city-level IP Geolocation is an important enabler for the modern digital ecosystem, underpinning services ranging from local content delivery and targeting to digital rights enforcement. However, traditional heuristic and database-driven methods often struggle to resolve the complex, non-linear allocation patterns of modern network infrastructures, particularly within the exploding IPv6 address space and transient mobile networks. In this paper, we introduce IPGeoAI, a novel deep learning model architecture that reframes geolocation from a static lookup problem to a sequential modeling task. Our approach utilizes the Transformer Encoder to capture hierarchical dependencies inherent in IP subnet structures. We propose a method to resolve geographic ambiguity by integrating unstructured semantic context via a Zero-Shot LLM Feature Extraction pipeline. We utilize Large Language Models to transform raw, noisy Autonomous Systems (AS) descriptions into structured, domain-specific metadata (such as 'University' vs. 'ISP' or 'Global' vs. 'Local') via an offline pre-computation process. By fusing these semantic signals into the network via a Multi-Head Cross-Attention module, we bridge the gap between numerical network topology and real-world semantic identity. Extensive offline evaluation on a proprietary dataset spanning 200,000 cities demonstrates that IPGeoAI significantly outperforms a leading external vendor in city-level granularity. By adopting a hierarchical inference strategy that refines coarse-grained country signals, our model achieves a 6% improvement in city-level accuracy while extending coverage to 100% of the traffic. Furthermore, in large-scale online production tests, the model drove a statistically significant +0.35% improvement in our 1st-tier downstream use cases metric.

</details>


### [97] [Reducing Hallucinated Transcripts in Whisper via Hallucination Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04561)
*Maryam Abbasihafshejani,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理时方法，通过低秩投影解码器激活值来抑制Whisper ASR模型在非语音输入上的幻觉生成，实现了92%以上的幻觉率相对降低，且提供了可控的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper作为广泛使用的ASR基础模型，其生成式解码器在包含极少或无语音的输入上会产生流利但虚假的转录文本（幻觉），这是一个严重的实际部署问题。现有方法通常需要重新训练或微调，成本高且可能影响正常语音识别性能。

Method: 提出基于低秩投影的推理时无训练方法：1) 从非语音校准数据中估计一个紧凑的幻觉相关子空间；2) 推理时将解码器隐藏状态投影远离该子空间。提供两种变体：always-on（对所有输入应用投影）和gated（仅当Whisper预测输入可能为非语音时应用）。

Result: 在非语音基准测试中，always-on投影将平均幻觉率(HR)从31.31%降至2.44%（相对降低92.21%），gated投影降至3.74%（相对降低88.05%），且gated版本对真实语音的误拒率更低。在LibriSpeech上，gated投影使绝对词错误率(WER)增加0.33-4.39个百分点，误拒率(FRR)为0.41-9.97%。

Conclusion: 低秩激活投影能在无需重训练的情况下大幅抑制Whisper幻觉，同时提供幻觉抑制与语音识别性能之间的可控权衡，gated变体在保持语音识别性能方面表现更优。

Abstract: Whisper is a widely used foundation model for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its generative decoder can produce fluent hallucinated transcripts for inputs containing little or no speech. We propose a training-free, inference-time method to reduce these hallucinations using low-rank projection of decoder activations. A compact hallucination-associated subspace is estimated from non-speech calibration data, and decoder hidden states are projected away from this subspace during inference.
  We evaluate two variants: always-on, which applies projection to all inputs, and gated, which applies it only when Whisper predicts that an input is likely non-speech. Across non-speech benchmarks, always-on projection reduces average hallucination rate (HR) from 31.31% to 2.44%, a 92.21% relative reduction, while gated projection reduces HR to 3.74%, an 88.05% relative reduction, with lower false rejection of genuine speech. On LibriSpeech, gated projection increases absolute word error rate (WER) by 0.33-4.39 percentage points and yields false-rejection rates (FRR) of 0.41--9.97% across model and split settings.
  These results show that low-rank activation projection can substantially suppress Whisper hallucinations without retraining, while providing a controllable trade-off between hallucination suppression and speech recognition performance.

</details>


### [98] [La Agente Óptima: Towards Agentic Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04564)
*Marcel Müller,Jiaru Bai,Willi Gottstein,Abhijoy Mandal,Mohammad Nazeri,Elia Savino,Yanlin Fang,Sujoy Das,Sergio Pablo García Carrillo,Yeonghun Kang,Juan B. Pérez-Sánchez,Simone Pilon,Martin Fitzner,Timothy Noël,Frank Gu,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 La Agente Óptima，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体框架，用于构建和监督贝叶斯优化活动，实现了自驱动实验室（SDL）中长期、可审计、无需专家设置的闭环优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自驱动实验室（SDL）的运行往往依赖人类专家将科学目标转化为可执行的闭环活动，并在数据和条件变化时调整。缺乏自动化的高层推理和持久状态管理，限制了 SDL 的普及和长期自主运行能力。

Method: 提出 La Agente Óptima 智能体框架，将 LLM 推理与执行活动分离，维持持久优化状态。LLM 仅在需要解释或修改活动时介入，其余重复优化循环由贝叶斯优化自动执行，所有决策可审计。框架支持跨计算和实验系统的闭环优化。

Result: 在消融研究、五个数字发现任务和两个物理平台上评估。接触角优化实验中，Óptima 识别并修正了中途测量故障，将接触角从 71.4° 降至 67.8°，推断目标范围（64-66°）可能不可达并建议改变配方。五天多目标流化学活动中，23 次实验将收率从 30% 提升至 59%，成本更低、耗料更少，且选择了更质量高效的操作点。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 的智能体能使严谨、长期运行的优化活动对领域科学家可及，无需专家设置，扩展了自驱动实验室的应用范围。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) combine automated experimentation with adaptive decision-making to accelerate scientific discovery. Their operation nevertheless often depends on human specialists who translate scientific objectives into executable closed-loop campaigns. Specialists adjust them as data and operating conditions change. Here, we present La Agente Óptima, an agentic framework that constructs and supervises Bayesian optimization campaigns across computational and experimental systems while maintaining a persistent optimization state. By separating large language model (LLM) reasoning from executed campaigns, Óptima runs repetitive optimization loops consistently, returns control to the agent only when progress requires interpretation or campaign revision, and keeps every decision auditable. We evaluate Óptima across ablation studies, five digital discovery tasks, and two physical platforms. Throughout, Óptima maintained executable campaigns as both the scientific problem and execution environment evolved. In a closed-loop contact angle optimization campaign, Óptima identified and corrected a mid-run measurement failure, bringing the contact angle from 71.4 to 67.8 degrees, just above the 64-66 degree range. From this result, Óptima correctly inferred that the target was likely unattainable with the available reagents and recommended changing the formulation. In a five-day multi-objective flow-chemistry campaign, Óptima increased the yield from 30% to 59% over 23 experiments. Despite substantial inference costs, it cost less and used substantially less starting material than a human-directed campaign, while selecting a more mass-efficient operating point. These results show that LLM-based agents can make rigorous, long-running optimization campaigns accessible to domain scientists without specialist setup, expanding the scope of SDLs.

</details>


### [99] [Extremely Sparse Supervision Incentivizes Reasoning Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04565)
*Zhishuai Liu,Xingzi Xu,Mehmet Saygin Seyfioglu,Pan Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了大语言模型后训练必须依赖密集token级监督的假设，发现仅在每条推理轨迹中使用1-2个token（约0.05%的token）进行稀疏监督，即可达到或超过全token训练的推理能力提升效果，这一现象在多种模型规模、任务类型和训练范式中均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法普遍假设有效学习需要大量token级监控，导致计算开销巨大。作者希望在On-policy distillation（OPD）这一天然提供密集教师监督的设定下，重新审视这一假设，探索极度稀疏监督是否足以激发模型推理能力。

Method: 基于Qwen3模型族，在数学推理任务上进行On-policy distillation实验，对比全token监督与极度稀疏监督（每条轨迹仅1-2个token，约0.05% token量）的效果；扩展验证至9种师生模型配置、代码推理任务、Llama模型及基于PPO的RLVR范式。

Result: 极度稀疏监督在大多数情况下匹配或超越全token训练的推理能力提升效果；该现象跨模型规模、任务类型（数学/代码推理）、模型架构（Qwen/Llama）及训练范式（OPD/PPO-RLVR）均一致成立。

Conclusion: 有效后训练不一定需要token密集型监督，极度稀疏监督可能更接近自然学习过程（仅反思关键步骤而非逐词纠错），为设计更高效的后训练算法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models demonstrate increasingly strong reasoning capabilities through effective post-training. Yet, prevailing post-training methods optimize over massive numbers of tokens, implicitly assuming that effective learning must be token-intensive. We revisit this assumption in the on-policy distillation (OPD) setting, which naturally admits dense teacher supervision at every generated token. Using the Qwen3 family, we discover a counter-intuitive phenomenon: reasoning can be effectively incentivized by an extremely small fraction of generated tokens--as few as one or two tokens per reasoning trajectory, corresponding to only 0.05% of all tokens. Surprisingly, this sparse supervision in most cases matches or surpasses full-token training in improving reasoning ability, despite excluding the vast majority of generated tokens from the training objective. This phenomenon is consistently observed across nine teacher--student configurations spanning different model scales on mathematical reasoning tasks, and is further validated on coding reasoning, Llama models and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR). Interestingly, such extremely sparse supervision may be closer to the natural learning process: rather than correcting every step word by word, one reflects on a few critical reasoning steps, updates prior understanding, and continues the trial-and-error, avoiding micro-level corrections while remaining remarkably effective. Overall, our results challenge the assumption that effective post-training must be token-intensive and point to a new direction for understanding and designing more efficient post-training algorithms.

</details>


### [100] [Does the Selected Object Reach the Reader? Auditing Identity Handoffs in Grounded Language-Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04579)
*Siddharth Vohra,Runmin Jiang,Xiaomo Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文审计了基于检索的语言模型管道中“对象选择—段落检索—证据回答”三阶段流程的对象传递可靠性，发现检索器对目标对象的召回率显著影响下游阅读器的精确匹配得分，并发布了可复现的 Returned-Object Profile (ROP) 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对落地语言模型管道中选定对象必须完整传递给阅读器，否则会导致交接失败的问题，现有基准仅检查数据集链接的对象，可能与实际选定对象不一致，因此需要系统审计选择器、检索器与阅读器之间的对象身份对齐及其对最终性能的影响。

Method: 在 600 道 HybridQA 问题上，跨三类选择器家族审计 1,463 条可解析记录：对比精确键查找、精确标题匹配、仅正文 BM25 与带重排序的混合检索在 cutoff-5 下的目标对象召回情况；统计两种对象身份（数据集追踪 vs 解码标题）的差异；冻结阅读器测量对齐对象存在与否对精确匹配的影响；并构建 64 项对照组实验，对比移除目标段落与移除同长度对照段落对性能的差异。

Result: 精确键查找与精确标题匹配均能 100% 召回目标对象；仅正文 BM25 在 cutoff-5 时漏掉 389 条 (26.6%)，混合检索+重排序仅漏 14 条 (1.0%)；两种对象身份在 1,792 条记录中有 329 条不一致，原始问题排序下 top-5 分歧 106 条 (5.9%)；冻结阅读器实验显示对齐对象存在时精确匹配提升 28.6–31.0 分；对照组实验证实移除目标段落显著降低精确匹配，而移除同长度对照段落不产生同等下降。

Conclusion: 检索阶段对目标对象的高召回率是保证端到端问答性能的关键；当前基准忽略选择器解码标题与数据集链接对象的身份差异，会低估检索失败对下游任务的影响；ROP 提供了可执行、可离线复现的完整对象返回画像，为后续管道优化提供了可靠的诊断工具。

Abstract: Grounded language-model pipelines can be divided into three stages: selecting an object, retrieving passages for it, and using that evidence to answer. If the selected object must reach the reader, losing it breaks the handoff. Benchmark recall checks the dataset-linked object, which can differ. We audit 600 HybridQA questions across three selector families. On 1,463 resolvable records where the selected object matches the dataset-traced passage, exact key lookup and exact title matching return the object every time. With every ranked rule given the same decoded selected title, body-only BM25 omits it on 389 records (26.6%) at cutoff five, while hybrid retrieval with reranking omits it on 14 (1.0%). The two identities differ on 329 of 1,792 resolvable records. With original-question rankings, their top-five checks disagree on 106 records (5.9%). Frozen reader comparisons associate the aligned object's presence with 28.6 to 31.0 points higher exact match. In a deliberately selected 64-item cohort, removing that passage sharply lowers exact match, while removing a similar-length comparison passage does not reproduce the drop. We release the Returned-Object Profile (ROP), an executable record of the target, returned-ID field, cutoff, membership rule, and complete expected population, with data and an offline replay.

</details>


### [101] [$τ^τ$-Bench: An Environment for End-To-End, Realistic Agent Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04611)
*Quan Shi,Keshav Dhandhania,Karthik Narasimhan,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 τ^τ-bench，一个让编码代理在真实客户约束下构建完整客服代理的基准，包含 53 个跨四领域任务。最强配置（Claude Opus 5 + Claude Code）仅通过 23.9% 测试，专家参考实现达 82.2%，差距主要来自浅层查询、客户沟通缺失与架构实验不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多评估现成代理的任务完成能力，鲜有衡量 AI 系统在真实交付场景中（给定业务记录、客户需求、生产 API、遗留代码库、成本与模型限制）从零构建生产级代理的能力。τ^τ-bench 旨在将协作式代理构建转化为可量化的编码代理目标。

Method: 基准提供：真实业务记录、持有需求的模拟客户、必须调用的生产 API、可继承的代码库、服务成本与模型预算。开发代理需输出完整客服代理，再在保留的模拟用户集上部署评分。共 53 任务覆盖四个领域，采用端到端模拟评估。

Result: 最强配置（Claude Opus 5 在 Claude Code 下）通过率仅 23.9%，专家编写的参考上限为 82.2%。典型失败模式包括：用浅层查询替代深度记录理解、几乎不与客户沟通、在架构与预算上实验不足就交付首个可运行版本。

Conclusion: τ^τ-bench 揭示了当前编码代理在真实交付条件下构建代理的巨大短板，为未来研究提供了可度量的协作式代理构建目标，推动从任务级评估向工程交付级评估迈进。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly becoming production software, deployed to handle customer service, adjudicate disputes, and operate internal systems. Notably, the work of building them is increasingly handed to coding agents, yet existing benchmarks say little about whether an AI system can deliver one under the conditions of a real client engagement. We introduce $τ^τ$-bench (pronounced hyper-tau-bench), a benchmark that makes agent construction the task. A developer agent is given the records a business actually keeps, a client who holds requirements, a production API that operations must run through, a codebase to inherit, and limits on serving cost and models: the same starting point a real engagement provides. From these it must deliver a complete customer-service agent, scored by deploying that agent against held-out simulated users. Across 53 tasks spanning four domains, the strongest configuration, Claude Opus 5 under Claude Code, passes just 23.9% of evaluation simulations. Meanwhile, an expert-authored reference ceiling scores 82.2%. The failures mirror ones human agent developers see: models issue shallow queries in place of deep comprehension of the records, communicate almost nothing to the client, and experiment too little with agent architecture and serving spend, shipping the first design that runs. We aim for $τ^τ$-bench to turn the work of cooperative agent building into a measurable target for coding agents.

</details>


### [102] [Leveraging Imperfect Restoration for Data Availability Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04627)
*Yi Huang,Jeremy Styborski,Mingzhi Lyu,Fan Wang,Adams Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为不完美恢复投毒（IRP）的新型数据投毒方法，通过理论分析现有CUDA方法的局限性，在保持高图像质量的同时实现强投毒效果，在监督学习和自监督学习场景下均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据可用性攻击（DAA）在监督学习（SL）或自监督学习（SSL）中往往只能在单一场景下表现优异；虽然基于卷积的不可学习数据集（CUDA）是跨场景最鲁棒的无模型攻击，但其在SSL下效果不佳，且面临图像质量与投毒效果的严重权衡。

Method: 通过理论分析CUDA引入的次优梯度及其诱导类别偏见的策略，提出不完美恢复投毒（IRP）方法，旨在保持高图像质量的同时实现强投毒效果。

Result: 在SL和SSL场景下与八种基线方法对比，并结合五种代表性防御方法评估，IRP展现出优越的投毒性能和图像质量保持能力。

Conclusion: IRP有效解决了CUDA在SSL下效果不佳及质量-效果权衡的问题，为跨场景数据保护提供了更优的无模型投毒方案。

Abstract: The abundance of online data is at risk of unauthorized usage in training deep learning models. To counter this, various Data Availability Attacks (DAAs) have been devised to make data unlearnable for such models by subtly perturbing the training data. However, existing attacks often excel against either Supervised Learning (SL) or Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) scenarios. Among these, a model-free approach that generates a Convolution-based Unlearnable Dataset (CUDA) stands out as the most robust DAA across both SSL and SL. Nonetheless, CUDA's effectiveness against SSL is underwhelming and it faces a severe trade-off between image quality and its poisoning effect. In this paper, we conduct a theoretical analysis of CUDA, uncovering the sub-optimal gradients it introduces and elucidating the strategy it employs to induce class-wise bias for data poisoning. Building on this, we propose a novel poisoning method named Imperfect Restoration Poisoning (IRP), aiming to preserve high image quality while achieving strong poisoning effects. Through extensive comparisons of IRP with eight baselines across SL and SSL, coupled with evaluations alongside five representative defense methods, we showcase the superiority of IRP. Code: https://github.com/lyumingzhi/IRP

</details>


### [103] [SiLR: Structure-Preserving Admission and Process Reward for LLM Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04629)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SiLR（基于乘积序的运行时门控），解决 LLM 工具代理在约束违规后的恢复准入问题。通过影子执行与分支级违规状态的乘积序（过载分支支持度与逐分支严重度）准入，证明标量投影在表示上不充分，在 Gym-ANM 与 CityLearn 基准上实现 21/21 多动作恢复，显著优于标量门控（9/21）与终端门控（0/21）；作为 GRPO 过程奖励亦全场景超越计数投影，且无门控策略超过未训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时门控多采用标量聚合分数，在违规后恢复场景中存在「标量投影陷阱」：接受局部改善的提议却将轨迹锁定在平台期，导致恢复失败。需一种能保持违规几何结构的准则，支持在仍处违规状态时仍能准入真正推进恢复的动作。

Method: SiLR 对每个提议进行影子执行，基于分支级违规状态构建乘积序：过载分支支持度（支持的约束分支数）与逐分支严重度（各分支违规程度）的元组比较。证明不存在合理的标量替代表征该偏序，避免阈值调优即可解决的误区。准入判定完全基于确定性仿真，LLM 置于信任边界之外。

Result: 在 24 个 Gym-ANM 场景中，SiLR 实现 21/21 多动作恢复，标量最佳门控 9/21，终端门控 0/21；结论在三大模型家族与 CityLearn 中一致。双约束族激活时，所有标量门控均准入物理不安全动作，仅支持度基线准入 63.2%，乘积序准入 0%。最难双族轨迹中，标量门控仅通过不安全动作恢复。作为 GRPO 过程奖励，SiLR 在所有挖掘场景超越计数投影，且其无门控策略得分 0.844 超过未训练基线 0.778。

Conclusion: 标量投影在设计时刻即丢失违规几何，仅完整乘积序在结构上充分。SiLR 通过分支级乘积序准入，实现了可证明可靠的违规后恢复，并能作为有效的过程奖励提升策略性能，展示了结构化准入对 LLM 代理安全性与鲁棒性的关键作用。

Abstract: A runtime gate for an LLM tool agent is usually cast as a filter. In a ReAct loop a rejected proposal is followed by another at the same state, so the gate is a search operator over the proposal stream whose admission criterion shapes which trajectories are reachable. We study post-violation recovery admission, where progress must be admitted while the system is still in violation, and identify the scalar projection trap: an aggregate-score gate accepts a locally improving proposal and commits the trajectory to a plateau. SiLR instead shadow-executes each proposal and admits it under a product order over the branch-level violation state (overloaded-branch support and per-branch severity). We prove that no scalar surrogate is sound for this order, so the failure is representational, not a matter of threshold tuning. On mined Gym-ANM scenarios, SiLR recovers 21/21 multi-action episodes against 0/21 for terminal and 9/21 for the best scalar gate, significant across the full 24-scenario benchmark. The terminal-versus-structured dichotomy holds across three model families and in CityLearn. Because admission rests on deterministic simulation, the LLM lies outside the trust boundary: a magnitude-redistribution attack that defeats both scalar and support-only baselines is contained only by the full per-branch predicate. With two constraint families active, every tested scalar projection admits physically unsafe actions; support-only admits the largest fraction (63.2% of 42,410; product order 0). In the hardest dual-family traces, scalar gates recover only through that unsafe class. Reused as a GRPO process reward, it outperforms its count projection in every mined scenario and is the only tested reward whose ungated policy exceeds the untrained base (0.844 vs. 0.778). Scalar projection loses the violation geometry at both design points; only the full product order is structurally sufficient.

</details>


### [104] [A Cost-Aware Agentic Architecture for NL-to-SQL over Nested Enterprise Schemas, with a New Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04641)
*Yoga Sri Varshan Varadharajan,Ajay Yadav,Ritesh Goru,Prateek Chaudhury,Constantine Caramanis,Prateek Jain,Divyateja Pasupuleti,Sunil Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DevRev NL2SQL基准（含900个执行验证查询和语义深度评分SDS）以及一种成本感知的单次生成代理架构，在该基准上达到91.7%准确率，领先基线54.6个百分点，并在Spider 2.0 Snowflake数据集上表现竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术基准无法捕捉生产级企业模式的图状、半结构化、深度嵌套特性，导致NL2SQL系统在实际部署中表现不足，亟需更贴近真实场景的评测体系和适配架构。

Method: 1) 构建DevRev NL2SQL基准：包含900个执行验证查询，涵盖嵌套类型和链接图结构，并设计模式无关的语义深度评分（SDS）量化分析推理深度。2) 提出成本感知单次生成代理架构：集成模式选择、元数据检索和错误修复模块，专为复杂企业模式需求设计。

Result: 在DevRev NL2SQL基准上达到91.7%答案正确率，比次优基线高出54.6个百分点；在Spider 2.0 Snowflake公开数据集上，单次生成模式下与领先系统表现相当。

Conclusion: DevRev基准填补了企业级复杂模式评测空白，SDS提供了可解释的推理深度度量；提出的代理架构通过单次生成即实现高准确度，显著降低推理成本，为生产级NL2SQL系统提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: Natural-language-to-SQL systems have ad- vanced rapidly on academic benchmarks, yet production enterprise schemas exhibit graph- like, semi-structured, deeply nested structure that current benchmarks do not measure. We make two complementary contributions. First, we introduce the DevRev NL2SQL bench- mark: 900 execution-verified queries with nested-type and link-graph structure, accom- panied by the Semantic Depth Score (SDS), a schema-agnostic rubric for analytical reasoning depth. Second, we present a cost-aware single- generation agentic architecture whose schema- selection, metadata-retrieval, and error-repair components are designed for the requirements this regime imposes. On the DevRev NL2SQL benchmark the system attains 91.7% answer correctness, a margin of 54.6 percentage points over the next-best baseline; on the Spider 2.0 Snowflake public dataset, it is competitive with leading systems at a single-generation operating point.

</details>


### [105] [Continual Graph Memory for Adaptive Recommendation under Intent Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04651)
*Hao Nguyen Ngoc,Tung Nguyen,Nguyen Thi Hanh,Hoang Thai Dinh,Nguyen Xuan Tung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CGM-Rec，一种用于自适应推荐的持续图记忆框架，通过将知识图谱视为可写内存，结合语义图记忆与情景教训记忆，在冻结模型参数的单遍重排协议下实现意图漂移下的自适应推荐，显著优于现有神经网络与大语言模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识图谱增强推荐系统将图谱视为静态检索底座，难以应对意图漂移、元数据噪声和反复失败模式。推荐反馈可揭示关系证据的有用、缺失或误导性，亟需一种能持续更新图谱状态、区分稳定知识与即时教训的自适应机制。

Method: CGM-Rec 包含两大互补记忆组件：1) 语义图记忆：通过质量门控的类型化操作保守更新，存储稳定高置信的关系知识；2) 情景教训记忆：作为快速反应记忆，学习近期结果、失败案例与修正提示。测试阶段模型参数冻结，仅通过内存写入实现自适应，采用冻结参数、单遍重排协议评估。

Result: 在多个推荐设置上，CGM-Rec 大多数指标优于神经网络与 LLM 基线。采样候选重排场景下，Bundle 数据集 HR@1 较最强 LLM 基线提升 29.58%；元数据丰富的 ML-100K 上 HR@5 达 0.5941，超越 K-RagRec 的 0.4746。

Conclusion: CGM-Rec 通过将知识图谱建模为可写的持续图记忆，有效解决了意图漂移下的自适应推荐问题。其双记忆架构兼顾长期稳定知识积累与短期快速经验学习，在冻结参数约束下实现了显著的性能提升，为动态推荐环境提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper studies adaptive recommendation under intent drift, where feedback from each recommendation outcome can reveal whether the relational evidence used for ranking is useful, missing, or misleading. While Knowledge Graphs (KGs) provide essential semantic structure to handle these shifts, traditional KG-enhanced systems treat the graph as a static retrieval substrate, making it brittle to evolving intents, noisy metadata, and recurring failure patterns. This paper proposes CGM-Rec, a continual graph memory framework for adaptive recommendation. CGM-Rec treats the graph state as a writable memory and maintains two complementary components. Therein, a Semantic Graph Memory is updated conservatively through quality-gated typed operations for storing stable and high-confidence relational knowledge. Meanwhile, an Episodic Lesson Memory acts as a fast reactive memory that learns recent outcomes, failure cases, and corrective hints. During testing, model parameters remain frozen and adaptation occurs only through memory writes. We evaluate CGM-Rec under a frozen-parameter, one-pass reranking protocol, where encoders and prompts remain fixed during testing and adaptation occurs only through memory writes. Experiments across multiple recommendation settings show that CGM-Rec improves over evaluated neural and LLM-based baselines on most metrics. Particularly, under sampled-candidate reranking, CGM-Rec improves HR@1 by up to 29.58% over the strongest LLM baseline on Bundle, and outperforms K-RagRec on metadata-rich ML-100K with HR@5 of 0.5941 versus 0.4746.

</details>


### [106] [Harness-agnostic detection and immunization of reward hacking in self-evolving language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04665)
*Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Haoxuan Jia,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Yingguang Yang,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HackProbe，一种针对自我进化语言模型的奖励黑客监测器，通过黑盒钩子监控模型演化，结合冻结分布的对比核心与轮换新鲜层，实现跨代理能力的可比性与抗共适应；四重检测配合 Sidak 校正给出校准的族级 p 值，并提供风险感知免疫层以有限比特预算重选诚实候选；理论给出可检测性界限，实验显示 AUROC 0.763 显著优于基线 0.663，误报率从 0.706 降至 0.434，且带宽受限的重选策略在黑客场景下净增真实能力 5.2 分，仅损失 4.7 分干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化语言模型通过候选更新与可见分数选择迭代，当代理分数与真实能力不完全对齐时，持续选择会导致奖励黑客，即代理分数与真实能力差距扩大。现有方法多需白盒访问或缺乏跨代理可比的监测机制，亟需一种黑盒、分布固定、可校准且具备免疫能力的通用监测框架。

Method: HackProbe 通过两个黑盒钩子接入自我进化循环：1) 秘密、分布固定的对比核心，冻结分布使能力代理跨代可比；2) 轮换新鲜层强化抗共适应。四大检测指标：层级差距、尺度对齐散度（含在线变点检测）、能力停滞、条件自信错误率，经 Sidak 校正得到族级校准 p 值。风险感知免疫层利用核心与结构化黑客足迹从候选池重选诚实候选，每代最多泄露 log2 Π 比特。理论推导可检测性界限，将目标误差率转化为显式探针规模预算，并界定轮换探针的得失。

Result: 在注入四条黑客通道且有真值标签的受控提示词级宿主上，HackProbe 达到 0.763 AUROC，显著优于最强基线 0.663；误报率从 0.706 降至 0.434。带宽受限的重选免疫是唯一在黑客场景下净增真实能力（平均 +5.2 分）且仅微损干净运行（-4.7 分）的免疫级别；单通道效应多不显著。

Conclusion: HackProbe 提供了一种无需白盒访问、理论有保证、实证有效的奖励黑客监测与免疫方案。其核心创新在于分布固定核心保证跨代理可比性、轮换层对抗共适应、多重检测族级校准、以及有限比特预算下的风险感知重选。实验验证了在受控环境下的优越检测性能与净能力增益，为自我进化系统的安全对齐提供了可落地的监管组件。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models improve by proposing candidate updates and keeping whatever raises a visible score. When that score is an imperfect proxy for the capability one actually wants, sustained selection widens the gap between the two. This is reward hacking. We introduce HackProbe, a monitor that attaches to an arbitrary self-evolving loop through two black-box hooks, with no access to weights or activations. It keeps a secret, distribution-fixed comparison core, whose frozen distribution makes its capability proxy comparable across generations, alongside a rotated fresh layer that hardens the bank against co-adaptation. Four tests built on that proxy cover the level gap, a scale-aligned divergence with online change-point detection, capability stagnation, and a conditional confidently-wrong rate; a Sidak correction turns them into a calibrated family-wise p-value. Diagnosis alone recovers nothing, so a risk-aware immunization layer reselects an honest candidate from the proposal pool using the core together with a purely structural gaming footprint, disclosing at most log2 Pi bits per generation to the host. We prove a detectability bound that converts a target error rate into an explicit probe-size budget, and we delimit what probe rotation does and does not buy. On a controlled prompt-level host with four injected hacking channels and ground-truth labels, HackProbe reaches 0.763 AUROC against 0.663 for the strongest baseline and cuts the false-positive rate from 0.706 to 0.434. Its bandwidth-limited reselection is the only immunization level that returns more true capability under hacking, 5.2 points on average, than it forfeits on clean runs, 4.7; per-channel effects are mostly not individually significant.

</details>


### [107] [ERPBench: Evaluating LLM Agents for Enterprise Decision-Making Across Competitive Market Ecologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04667)
*Xinran Zhang,Pengrui Lu,Lyumanshan Ye,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ERPBench，一个用于评估企业决策 LLM 代理的执行插桩基准，包含耦合定价、生产、采购、库存、财务及共享市场竞争的六轮 ERP 模拟。通过在 Solo（对抗固定规则对手）和 Arena（六代理共享市场）两种竞争生态中对同 100 固定问题进行配对评估，发现不同生态下的最优模型不同（Solo 中 DeepSeek 领先，Arena 中 Gemini 领先），且仅 21% 任务级胜者一致，揭示企业代理排序难以跨竞争生态迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理评估很少测试业务决策结论是否能跨竞争市场生态迁移，缺乏在耦合多业务模块且包含共享市场竞争的真实企业环境中的系统性基准。

Method: 构建 ERPBench：六轮 ERP 模拟，集成定价、生产、采购、库存、财务及共享市场竞争。设置两种匹配的竞争生态：Solo（单代理对抗固定规则对手）和 Arena（六代理共享市场）。对六个模型家族各跑 100 固定问题，共 1,200 模型级轨迹、7,200 决策轮。采用配对评估比较跨生态排名迁移性，并辅以聚合执行干预分析。

Result: Solo 中 DeepSeek 平均估值 2.5229 亿、平均排名 1.67 领先；Arena 中 Gemini 平均估值 2.6395 亿、平均排名 1.76 领先。两生态仅在 21/100 问题上认出相同任务级胜者。Gemini 在 Solo 中垫底率 22%，在 Arena 降为 0%。表明代理排名高度依赖竞争生态，难以直接迁移。

Conclusion: ERPBench 证明企业决策代理的性能排名随竞争市场生态显著变化，单一生态评估不足以反映真实部署表现。需采用配对跨生态基准（如 Solo 与 Arena）进行更稳健的企业代理评估。代码与基准资源已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed for enterprise workflows, yet existing evaluations rarely test whether business-decision conclusions transfer across competitive market ecologies. We introduce ERPBench, an execution-instrumented benchmark for enterprise decision agents in a six-round Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) simulation with coupled pricing, production, procurement, inventory, finance, and shared-market competition. ERPBench evaluates the same 100 fixed problems in two matched competitive market ecologies: Solo, where each evaluated LLM agent competes against fixed rule-based opponents, and Arena, where six evaluated LLM agents compete in a shared market. Across six model families, this yields 1,200 model-level trajectories spanning 7,200 decision rounds. Under the observed service configuration, the leading model differs between ecologies: DeepSeek leads in Solo (252.29M mean valuation; mean rank 1.67), whereas Gemini leads in Arena (263.95M; 1.76). The two ecologies identify the same task-level winner on only 21 of 100 problems, and Gemini's bottom-rank rate falls from 22 % to 0 % in Arena. ERPBench supports paired evaluation of whether enterprise-agent rankings transfer across competitive market ecologies, supplemented by aggregate execution-intervention analysis. Code and benchmark resources are available in our https://github.com/GAIR-NLP/erp-bench.

</details>


### [108] [Train What You Deploy:Token-Faithful Post-Training of a Production Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04678)
*Cheng Li,Jiexiong Liu,Yixuan Chen,Chi Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保真度感知的训练耦合框架和Certified Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization (C-DPPO)，解决了编码和终端代理后训练流程中的token和控制保真度错误，在Baize5B和Baize10B模型上实现了+3.0点的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练流程存在严重的token和控制保真度错误：简化的训练环境与生产部署不匹配，从代理日志离线重构token会扭曲原始提示并混淆策略调用与后台模型操作。

Method: 提出保真度感知的训练耦合框架，保留训练端对原始提示的采样，通过协商训练协议消除虚假模型调用，将损失计算限制在可验证的token跨度并提供闭合失败保证。进一步提出C-DPPO，建立紧致的双侧TV认证边界、自适应K规则、预算感知序列保证和错误鲁棒策略掩码。

Result: 在相同训练和测试协议的TMax-100基准上，C-DPPO在Baize5B和Baize10B模型上均比标准DPPO持续提升+3.0点性能。证书审计验证了认证训练管道的可靠性和全操作覆盖。

Conclusion: C-DPPO通过理论认证的保真度保证和实用的训练协议，有效解决了代理后训练中的分布偏移和token重构失真问题，为生产级代理训练提供了可靠的框架。

Abstract: Existing post-training pipelines for coding and terminal agents suffer severe token and control fidelity errors: simplified training environments mismatch production deployments, and offline token reconstruction from agent logs distorts original prompts and conflates policy calls with background model operations. We present a fidelity-aware training coupling framework that retains trainer-side sampling over original prompts, eliminates spurious model calls via a negotiated training protocol, and restricts loss computation to verifiable token spans with closed-failure guarantees. We further propose Certified Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization (C-DPPO), which establishes tight two-sided TV certification bounds, adaptive-K rules, budget-aware sequence guarantees, and error-robust policy masking atop standard DPPO. Evaluated on matched Baize5B and Baize10B models with identical training and test protocols on TMax-100, C-DPPO yields a consistent +3.0-point performance gain over standard DPPO across model scales. Certificate audits validate the reliability and full operational coverage of our certified training pipeline.

</details>


### [109] [Predicting Spatiotemporal Mobile Sensing-Based PM2.5 Concentrations Using Low-Rank Adapted Spatially Attentive Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04693)
*Om Chiddarwar,Priyanka Mandal,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Shriniwas Arkatkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于移动传感数据的SA-GNN模型，用于城市PM2.5的精细化短期预测与热点识别，结合DBSCAN聚类与图注意力网络，显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通走廊空气质量沿途变化大，需要高分辨率监测；现有模型在低分辨率数据上表现尚可，但难以捕捉城市空气质量的快速时空变化模式。

Method: 采集印度苏拉特移动传感数据集（PM2.5、气象、土地利用）；用均匀分段（200-400米）和DBSCAN聚类两种节点定义策略构建时空图；对每个节点计算气象变量的滚动均值与标准差；提出SA-GNN：簇特定GRU捕捉局部时序依赖，图注意力网络学习空间异质性。

Result: 在自建数据集上，SA-GNN达到R²=0.95，RMSE=6.8 μg/m³，MAE=4.2 μg/m³，全面超越LSTM、RNN、GRU、ANN等基线模型。

Conclusion: 空间聚类与自适应注意力机制的结合显著提升精细化预测性能，可支持实时高分辨率监测、个性化暴露追踪及及时预警，助力健康城市建设。

Abstract: Urban air quality can vary significantly along transit corridors, necessitating high-resolution monitoring. This work introduces a novel mobile-sensing dataset from Surat, Gujarat, India, comprising PM$*{2.5}$ concentrations, meteorological variables (temperature, humidity, wind speed, wind direction), and land-use features. To represent the spatiotemporal data as a graph, two node-definition strategies were used: (i) uniform segmentation (200--400~m intervals) and (ii) DBSCAN clustering to adaptively group dense observations. For each node, rolling mean and standard deviation of meteorological variables were computed. To model this high-dimensional data, we propose a SA-GNN for fine-grained, short-term PM$*{2.5}$ forecasting and hotspot identification. We compared SA-GNN with LSTM, RNN, GRU, and ANN models. These models performed well on low-resolution data but had difficulty capturing rapidly changing patterns in urban air quality. SA-GNN employs cluster-specific GRUs to capture localized temporal dependencies and a Graph Attention Network to learn spatial heterogeneity. This hybrid architecture effectively models rapid fluctuations and complex spatial interactions. On our dataset, SA-GNN achieved $R^2 = 0.95$, RMSE $= 6.8$, and MAE $= 4.2~\si{\micro\gram\per\meter\cubed}$, outperforming all baseline models. Combining spatial clustering with adaptive attention significantly improves forecasting, enabling real-time, fine-grained monitoring and supporting personalized exposure tracking and timely alerts for healthier cities.

</details>


### [110] [SQL-Zero: Self-Evolving Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04697)
*Daniel Machado Pedrozo,Julia Soares Dollis,Bryan Lincoln Marques de Oliveira,Vinicius Alboneti Aguiar,Sávio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira,Telma Woerle de Lima Soares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SQL-Zero 通过 proposer-solver 自博弈训练 Text-to-SQL 求解器，仅依赖数据库执行反馈作为真值，无需人工标注 SQL。在 BIRD 基准上，3B 和 7B 模型分别比零样本基线提升 6.6 和 7.3 分；且优于同配方在人工标注 BIRD gold 上训练的对照组。迁移性能随规模变化：3B 每轮迭代都提升在 Spider 和 Spider-Syn 上的表现且退化较小，7B 仅首轮保持迁移优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 训练高度依赖昂贵、领域特定的人工标注 NL-SQL 对，难以扩展到新数据库。作者旨在探索完全无标注训练，仅利用数据库执行结果作为监督信号，实现竞争性求解器的自举训练。

Method: 提出 SQL-Zero：同一基础 LLM 初始化 challenger（提议者）和 solver（求解器）两角色；challenger 生成针对 solver 当前难度的 SQL 对（瞄准“难但可解”）；双角色轮流用 GRPO 更新；challenger 引入模板级重复惩罚防止多样性坍塌；唯一真值为数据库执行反馈。

Result: 在 BIRD dev 上，零样本基线提升 6.6 分（3B）和 7.3 分（7B）；且高于同配方在人工 BIRD gold 上训练的对照组（虽配对检验未达显著）。迁移方面：3B 每轮迭代均优于基线于 unseen Spider 及 Spider-Syn，且退化幅度小于对照组；7B 仅首轮保持迁移优势。

Conclusion: SQL-Zero 验证了纯自博弈、仅凭执行反馈即可训练出强 Text-to-SQL 模型，消除人工标注瓶颈。小规模模型（3B）展现出更稳健的迁移与抗扰动能力；大规模模型（7B）虽在源域收益更大，但迁移鲁棒性随迭代下降，提示规模与自博弈动态的复杂交互。

Abstract: Training a competitive Text-to-SQL agent usually depends on human-annotated natural-language/SQL pairs, which are expensive, domain-specific, and a bottleneck for scaling to new databases. We show it is possible to train a competitive solver with zero annotated pairs. We introduce SQL-Zero, a proposer-solver self-play in which a challenger and a solver start from the same base LLM and the only ground truth is execution against the database itself. The challenger generates SQL pairs calibrated to the solver's current difficulty (targeting "hard but solvable"), and both roles are updated with GRPO in alternating turns, with a template-level repetition penalty on the challenger to prevent diversity collapse. Training on BIRD databases with no labels, self-play improves over the zero-shot base on BIRD dev by 6.6 points at 3B and 7.3 points at 7B. It also scores higher than a matched control trained under the same recipe on human BIRD gold over the same databases, although an exact paired test does not resolve that margin. Transfer depends on scale: at 3B every iteration outperforms the base on unseen Spider databases and under lexical perturbation (Spider-Syn), where it also degrades less than the matched BIRD-gold control, whereas at 7B only the first iteration preserves transfer.

</details>


### [111] [Model Retirement Creates Reproducibility Risk in Biomedical AI Publications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04699)
*Nathan Wolfrath,Meghan Conroy,Thomas Kosten,Dave Bell,Bhabishya Neupane,Jonah Kindel,Anjishnu Banerjee,Priya Deshpande,Bradley Taylor,Anai N. Kothari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究分析了2022年至2026年3月期间PubMed中使用大语言模型（LLM）的生物医学论文，发现77.7%引用的是商业闭源模型，42%的论文使用的模型在发表时已退役或将在两年内退役，发表到模型退役的中位间隔为538天，表明许多基于LLM的生物医学研究面临计算不可复现的风险，模型弃用应被视为核心报告与保存问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生物医学研究中快速普及，但托管这些模型的商业服务存在弃用计划，可能危及科学可复现性。研究旨在量化生物医学文献中LLM使用的模型生命周期问题，评估模型弃用对研究复现性的影响。

Method: 检索2022年至2026年3月PubMed原创研究文章，使用提取代理从61,077篇摘要中识别模型名称，人工审核验证准确性，将模型名标准化为规范标识符，汇编前50个最常用模型的生命周期数据（发布日期、退役日期、状态）。

Result: 共识别出8,931个论文-模型提及，涵盖5,242篇唯一出版物（限前50模型）。其中77.7%引用商业闭源模型。42%的提及涉及发表时已退役或计划两年内退役的模型。发表到模型退役的中位间隔为538天。

Conclusion: 大量使用LLM的生物医学出版物发表后将面临计算不可复现的风险。模型弃用应作为生物医学研究的核心报告和保存问题处理，需建立模型版本归档、报告规范及长期访问机制以保障可复现性。

Abstract: Background. Large language models (LLMs) are being adopted in biomedical research at a rapid and accelerating pace, yet commercial services that host many widely used models operate under deprecation schedules that can complicate scientific reproducibility.
  Methods. We searched PubMed for original research articles from 2022 through March 2026 that applied a specific LLM to a biomedical task. An extraction agent identified model names from 61,077 article abstracts with human reviewers validating a subset for extraction accuracy. Extracted model names were normalized to canonical model identifiers. Lifecycle data (release date, retirement date, status) were compiled for the 50 most frequently used models.
  Results. We identified 8,931 paper-model mentions spanning 5,242 unique publications after restricting the analysis to the 50 most frequently used models. Among these mentions, 77.7% cited a commercial closed-weight model. Overall, 42% involved a model that was already retired by the time of official publication or is scheduled to retire within two years of publication. The median interval from publication to model retirement was 538 days.
  Conclusion. Many biomedical publications using LLMs are on a trajectory toward computational non-reproducibility after publication. Model deprecation should be treated as a core reporting and preservation issue for biomedical research.

</details>


### [112] [FinalityBench: An Effect-Level Benchmark for Agent Decisions Under Delayed and Conflicting Financial Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04706)
*Abhishek Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinalityBench，一个针对商家支付处理中多系统状态不一致决策的可执行基准。通过模拟消息延迟、重复、丢失、乱序导致的四系统视图分歧，评估智能体在不可逆操作（发货、重新捕获、退款、等待）中的决策质量。基准包含321个任务，含45对孪生任务（视图相同但最终正确处置不同）。实验显示：基于权威最终性探针的运行时门控策略达85.4%准确率且无额外损失；语言模型能自主发现该策略但损失约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商家支付处理器、账本、ERP和银行流因消息传递故障（延迟、重复、丢失、乱序）导致对同一订单持有相互矛盾的信念，持续数分钟。异常处理代理必须在不可逆操作（发货、重新捕获、退款、等待）中决策，缺乏标准化评估基准。

Method: 构建FinalityBench：维护隐藏的规范事件日志，为每个系统生成独立故障注入的消息流以派生其视图，分歧源于指定的故障语义而非人工编造。评分基于执行后的货币效应：以商家终局经济头寸相对于知晓捕获最终结果的特权参考为准。语料库含321任务，含45对孪生任务（决策瞬间四系统视图相同、权威探针均返回未知、最终正确处置不同），快照不可区分性在每个评估种子下验证。测试9个程序化策略共14,445个分级回合，对比单任务准确率与配对损失排名。

Result: 单任务准确率与配对损失排名在7处分歧：首单即发策略准确率次优（65.7%）但配对损失最差，因无法区分孪生任务。基于权威最终性探针的运行时门控不可逆动作策略达85.4%准确率，且与所有轮询策略不同，无pass^5损失；剩余损失几乎全属一类型（直接为最终性信息定价）。语言模型在分层子集上达到与手写门控相同的准确率，但损失约两倍资金，且未被告知的情况下自主发现最终性门控策略。

Conclusion: FinalityBench为支付异常决策提供了严谨、可复现的基准，揭示了准确率指标的局限性（无法区分孪生任务），证明了最终性探针门控策略的有效性，并展示了语言模型自主发现该策略的潜力。残余损失主要源于最终性信息的定价成本，指明了未来改进方向。

Abstract: A merchant's payment processor, ledger, ERP and bank feed are updated by messages that get delayed, duplicated, dropped and reordered, so for minutes at a time the four hold contradictory beliefs about the same order. An agent resolving the exception must decide whether to ship goods, re-submit a capture, refund or wait, knowing some of those cannot be undone. We present FinalityBench, an executable benchmark for that decision. It keeps a hidden canonical event log and derives each system's view from a separately faulted delivery stream, so disagreement follows from specified fault semantics rather than being authored. Grading is on executed monetary effects: an episode is scored by the merchant's terminal economic position, relative to a privileged reference told when the pending capture resolves. The corpus of 321 tasks includes 45 twin pairs (90 tasks): tasks whose four system views are identical at the decision instant, whose authoritative probes both return unknown, and whose eventual correct dispositions differ. That snapshot indistinguishability is checked under every evaluation seed rather than assumed; equivalence over all interaction traces is not claimed. Over 14,445 graded episodes from nine programmatic policies, ranking by single-task accuracy and by paired loss disagree in 7 places: a ship-on-first-sign policy is second-best by accuracy at 65.7% and worst in the suite by paired loss, because it cannot tell the two members apart. A runtime gating irreversible actions on an authoritative finality probe reaches 85.4% and, unlike every polling policy, loses nothing to pass^5; its residual loss is almost entirely one archetype, which prices finality information directly. Language models reach the same exact rate as the hand-written gate on a stratified subset, lose about twice as much money, and discover the finality-gating strategy without being told it.

</details>


### [113] [PLUME: Parameter-Efficient Personalization of Large Language Models via Low-Rank User Modulation in Shared Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04715)
*Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Julina Maharjan,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Ruoming Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PLUME 框架，通过共享任务特定子空间实现轻量级的大语言模型个性化，仅需训练每用户极小的方阵，将参数量减少 95% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐用户微调虽能显著提升个性化质量，但引入巨大的参数和存储开销，难以扩展到大规模用户群体。

Method: PLUME 先从聚合用户数据学习全局任务子空间，个性化仅在该子空间内训练一个轻量级小方阵，共享组件保持固定；再引入跨层共享参数和秩-1 残差项，进一步降低冗余并保持表达能力。

Result: 在多个个性化文本生成基准上，PLUME 性能与强基线持平或更优，同时将逐用户参数量降低 95% 以上。

Conclusion: 基于共享子空间的调制配合最小残差，是一种可扩展且语义落地的大语言模型个性化范式。}

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) is essential for delivering AI assistance that aligns with individual users' styles, intents, and preferences. While per-user fine-tuning can substantially enhance personalization quality, it introduces significant parameter and storage overhead, limiting scalability to large user populations. We propose PLUME (Personalized Low-Rank Adaptation through User Modulation and Shared Subspace), a lightweight framework that achieves efficient and expressive per-user adaptation by leveraging a shared task-specific subspace. Specifically, PLUME first learns a global task subspace from aggregated user data. Personalization is then achieved by training only a lightweight small square matrix within this subspace, enabling each user to obtain a tailored model while keeping shared components fixed. Cross-layer shared parameters and rank-1 residual terms are further introduced to significantly reduce redundancy while maintaining expressiveness. Experiments on multiple personalized text generation benchmarks demonstrate that PLUME achieves comparable or superior performance to strong baselines, while reducing per-user parameters by over 95%. These results establish shared-subspace modulation with minimal residuals as a scalable and semantically grounded approach to LLM personalization.

</details>


### [114] [Aplaud: Adaptive Personalized Low-Rank Decomposition for User-Specific LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04738)
*Xinyu Li,Ruoming Jin,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Zhi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Aplaud 框架，通过冻结共享低秩基矩阵与用户特定校正矩阵的解耦分解，结合秩一残差与进一步低秩因子化，实现大语言模型（LLM）的个性化调查回答预测，显著降低每用户参数量并优于现有 LoRA 个性化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对个性化调查回答预测中面临的三大挑战：单用户训练数据稀缺、模型存储扩展性受限、以及跨问题共享结构利用不足，需要一种轻量且可扩展的 LLM 个性化框架。

Method: Aplaud 扩展 LoRA 范式：将适应过程分解为冻结的共享低秩基矩阵、紧凑的用户特定校正矩阵，并引入秩一残差实现更细粒度的个性化；进一步将校正矩阵因子化为更低秩形式，以减少每用户参数并缓解过拟合。

Result: 实验表明，Aplaud 在多用户场景下实现了高效、可扩展的个性化，泛化性能与推理效率均优于当前最先进的基于 LoRA 的个性化 LLM 方法。

Conclusion: Aplaud 通过共享低秩基与用户特定低秩校正的嵌套分解，兼顾了参数效率、存储可扩展性与个性化表达能力，为 LLM 在小样本、多用户任务上的部署提供了实用范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of personalized survey response prediction using fine-tuned large language models (LLMs). This task poses unique challenges: limited per-user training data, scalability of model storage, and the need to exploit shared structure across survey questions. To address these issues, we propose Aplaud (Adaptive Personalized Low-rank and User-specific Nested Decomposition), a lightweight and scalable framework for LLM personalization. Aplaud extends the LoRA paradigm by separating adaptation into a frozen, shared low-rank basis and a compact user-specific correction, augmented with a rank-one residual for finer personalization. To further reduce per-user parameter cost and mitigate overfitting, the correction matrix can be factorized into an even lower-rank form. Empirical results demonstrate that Aplaud achieves efficient, scalable personalization across users while outperforming state-of-the-art LoRA-based personalized LLM approaches in both generalization and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [115] [DCFA: Dual-view Causal-inspired Attribution for Failure Reasoning in LLM-based Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04749)
*Zehao Wang,Lanjun Wang,Shilong Jin,Junjie Chen,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCFA是一个无需训练的故障归因框架，通过全局因果依赖图构建和局部反事实推理，解决多智能体系统中浅层归因和上下文退化问题，在Who&When基准上将步级准确率提升高达8.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统脆弱，推理和协调错误常导致系统级故障。现有故障归因方法存在两大挑战：1) 浅层归因：仅捕捉次要偏差（如检索不全、格式错误），遗漏决定性错误；2) 上下文退化：随着系统轨迹变长，模型推理能力急剧下降。

Method: DCFA包含两个模块：全局模块从系统轨迹构建结构化因果启发式依赖图，识别初始决定性错误；局部模块应用局部反事实启发式推理，细化因果启发式归因。该框架无需训练。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，跨六个大语言模型实验，DCFA相对于最先进基线将步级准确率提升高达8.27%。

Conclusion: DCFA有效解决了多智能体系统故障归因中的浅层归因和上下文退化问题，通过因果依赖图和反事实推理的结合，显著提升了归因准确性，为构建更可靠的多智能体系统提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems have experienced rapid growth in recent years. Despite their promise, such systems remain fragile, frequently exhibiting reasoning and coordination errors that can lead to system-level failures. Failure attribution in such systems relies on tracing natural language interactions among agents to identify the decisive error, which refers to the earliest action whose correction can reverse system failure. There are two key challenges: 1) Shallow attribution: Existing methods often capture only minor deviations, such as incomplete retrievals or formatting errors, which verification mechanisms can correct, while missing the decisive cause of system failure. 2) Contextual degradation: As the length of the system traces increases, the model's reasoning ability rapidly deteriorates. To address these challenges, we propose DCFA, a training-free framework for failure attribution. DCFA integrates a global module that constructs structured causal-inspired dependency graphs from system traces to identify the initial decisive error, and a local module that applies local counterfactual-inspired reasoning to refine causal-inspired attribution. Experiments on the Who&When benchmark across six LLMs show that DCFA improves step-level accuracy by up to 8.27% over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [116] [Shadow Queries for Private Retrieval in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04767)
*Xinguo Feng,Zhongkui Ma,Zihan Wang,Chuan Yan,Guowei Yang,Alsharif Abuadbba,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHAQ（影子查询生成）防御嵌入反演攻击（EIA），通过语义分解和嵌入解耦，用生成的影子查询嵌入替代原始文档嵌入，在大幅提升隐私保护的同时保持检索效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越依赖检索增强生成（RAG）系统，这些系统将预计算的文档嵌入存储在云端向量数据库中。然而，嵌入易受嵌入反演攻击（EIA），攻击者可从嵌入重建原始文本。现有防御（如添加噪声或缩放嵌入）往往隐私保护有限或严重降低检索效用。

Method: SHAQ基于EIA依赖嵌入与其原始文本强耦合的洞察。不直接存储文档嵌入，而是使用生成式语言模型为每个文档创建多样化的影子查询，捕捉文档不同语义方面。将这些查询编码并存储，替代原始文档嵌入，从而分解文档语义并解耦存储嵌入与源文本。

Result: 在多样化IR数据集上的实验显示，SHAQ显著提升隐私同时保持检索效用：恢复率低至0.2104，比基线防御多保护19.50%的token，MAP@10高达0.7967，效用提升达5.53%。

Conclusion: 语义分解和嵌入解耦为防御嵌入反演攻击提供了一种有效替代方案，优于直接修改嵌入的方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on information retrieval (IR) systems, such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), to incorporate domain-specific knowledge without costly re-training. These systems often store pre-computed document embeddings in cloud-based vector databases. However, such embeddings are vulnerable to embedding inversion attacks (EIAs), which can reconstruct their underlying text. Existing defenses, such as adding noise or scaling embeddings, often provide limited privacy or significantly reduce retrieval utility.
  We propose SHAQ (shadow query generation), a semantic-decomposition and embedding-decoupling defense against EIAs. SHAQ is based on the insight that EIAs rely on the strong coupling between an embedding and its original text. Instead of storing document embeddings directly, SHAQ uses a generative language model to create diverse shadow queries that capture different semantic aspects of each document. These queries are then encoded and stored in place of the original document embeddings, thereby decomposing document semantics and decoupling stored embeddings from the source text.
  Experiments across diverse IR datasets show that SHAQ substantially improves privacy while preserving retrieval utility, achieving a recovery rate as low as 0.2104, defending up to 19.50% more tokens than baseline defenses, and reaching up to 0.7967 MAP@10 with up to 5.53% utility improvement. These results demonstrate that semantic decomposition and embedding decoupling provide an effective alternative to directly modifying embeddings for defending against EIAs.

</details>


### [117] [Diffusion Language Models for Mobile Edge Agentic AI: Foundations, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04778)
*Chenqi Li,Minghui Min,Dusit Niyato,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了扩散语言模型(DLMs)在移动边缘智能体AI中的应用潜力,分析其并行去噪、双向上下文、质量-延迟弹性等特性如何满足边缘计算的延迟、内存、能耗、带宽、隐私和可靠性约束,涵盖架构、训练推理加速、压缩、部署、通信感知服务、IoT应用及评测,并指出长上下文管理、分割推理、可信执行、多模态落地、可复现基准等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLMs的逐token顺序解码在边缘场景下面临延迟高、难以灵活权衡质量与速度、对噪声/不完整/动态上下文鲁棒性差等挑战。DLMs通过迭代并行去噪、双向上下文建模,支持部分细化、早退出、约束引导修正,能更好适配边缘智能体对低延迟、低通信开销、高鲁棒性的需求。

Method: 系统性综述方法:1)梳理DLMs基础理论与建模特性(双向性、并行细化、可控性、质量-延迟弹性);2)从资源高效架构、训练/推理加速、模型压缩、边云协同部署、通信感知服务、IoT/无线应用、智能体评测七个维度分析DLMs在边缘端的适配性;3)结合边缘系统约束(延迟/内存/能耗/带宽/隐私/可靠性)评估权衡;4)归纳长上下文状态管理、分割推理、可信执行、多模态落地、可复现基准测试五大开放问题。

Result: DLMs凭借非自回归并行去噪机制,在边缘端展现出更灵活的质量-延迟权衡、更低的首token延迟潜力、对噪声/动态上下文的更强鲁棒性,且天然支持约束引导生成。现有工作已在架构轻量化、蒸馏/量化压缩、边云分割推理、通信感知调度等方面取得进展,但在长上下文状态维护、分布式可信执行、多模态对齐、统一评测基准等方面仍存在显著缺口。

Conclusion: DLMs的建模特性与移动边缘智能的系统级需求高度契合,是未来边缘智能体AI的重要候选范式。需重点攻关长上下文高效状态管理、分割推理通信优化、可信执行框架、多模态扩散建模、可复现标准化基准等核心问题,推动DLMs从理论优势向边缘落地效能转化。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a non-autoregressive alternative for mobile edge agentic artificial intelligence (AI) by refining tokens through iterative denoising rather than left-to-right decoding. Compared with autoregressive Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), DLMs can update multiple uncertain tokens in parallel and exploit bidirectional context throughout the generation process, enabling more flexible quality-latency trade-offs beyond fixed sequential decoding. These properties are particularly attractive for edge agents, where partial refinement, early exit, and constraint-guided correction can reduce response delay and communication overhead while improving robustness under noisy, incomplete, or dynamic contexts. This survey reviews DLM foundations and analyzes their suitability for edge settings under latency, memory, energy, bandwidth, privacy, and reliability constraints. We cover resource-efficient architectures, training and inference acceleration, compression, edge/cloud deployment, communication-aware serving, Internet of Things (IoT)/wireless applications, and evaluation of DLM-based agents. We further discuss open issues in long-context state management, split inference, trustworthy execution, multimodal grounding, and reproducible benchmarking. The goal is to connect DLM modeling properties, including bidirectionality, parallel refinement, controllability, and quality-latency elasticity, with system-level requirements of future mobile edge intelligence.

</details>


### [118] [DODR: Deterministic Operator-Driven Reasoning in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04782)
*Weicai Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出确定性算子驱动潜在空间推理架构（DODR），将推理重构为高维线性代数空间中的推理图计算，用语义单元快照向量替代token，用确定性矩阵运算替代概率采样，形式化皮尔士三推理类型为三个可训练矩阵算子，实现零幻觉、可证明收敛的结构化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型将推理建模为token级概率采样，导致三大根本缺陷：误差累积、以概率替代必然性、线性链信息瓶颈，无法胜任复杂逻辑推理。

Method: 构建DODR架构：推理状态表示为语义单元（短语/句子）级快照向量，每步推理为确定性矩阵运算。形式化三算子：秩亏演绎算子（信息塌缩）、满秩归纳算子（信息扩展）、作为演绎Moore-Penrose广义逆的溯因算子（信息假设）。证明算子集在皮尔士三分法下极小完备，单一超算子因秩障碍不可行，推理图在收缩回流下由Banach不动点定理保证收敛且图灵完备。提供三层持续学习机制。

Result: 在503条记录（420去重样本）实验中：演绎损失收敛至1.40e-05；归纳泛化覆盖率0.9996，20/20硬否决反例；溯因方案超随机基线28倍，判断准确率72.5%（58/80，Wilson 95% CI [61.9%,81.1%]）和81.7%（49/60，CI [70.1%,89.4%]）；冻结算子在未见跨域演绎达100%（60/60）。

Conclusion: DODR提供结构化零幻觉保证，通过确定性线性代数重构推理，解决了自回归模型的根本缺陷，实现了可证明正确、可泛化、可持续学习的复杂逻辑推理。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) large language models formulate reasoning as token-level probabilistic sampling, which induces three fundamental defects in complex logical reasoning: error accumulation, probability substituting necessity, and the linear-chain information bottleneck. This paper proposes the Deterministic Operator-Driven Reasoning in Latent Space architecture (DODR), which reconstructs reasoning as reasoning-graph computation in a high-dimensional linear-algebraic space. Reasoning states are represented as snapshot vectors whose primitives are semantic units (phrases or sentences) rather than tokens, and each inference step is a deterministic matrix operation with no token sampling. Peirce's three inference types are formalized as three trainable matrix operators: a rank-deficient deduction operator (information collapse), a full-rank induction operator (information expansion), and an abduction operator defined as the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of deduction (information hypothesizing). We prove that the operator set is minimal and complete given Peirce's trichotomy, that no single "super-operator" can realize all three types (a rank obstruction), and that reasoning graphs are Turing-complete with contractive backflow converging by Banach's fixed-point theorem. Experiments on 503 sample records (420 deduplicated samples) across dedicated and end-to-end settings show: deduction loss converges to 1.40e-05; induction achieves 0.9996 generalization coverage with 20/20 hard vetoes on counterexamples; abduction solutions exceed the random baseline by 28x with judgment accuracies of 72.5% (58/80, Wilson 95% CI [61.9%, 81.1%]) and 81.7% (49/60, CI [70.1%, 89.4%]); frozen operators attain 100% (60/60) on unseen cross-domain deduction. The architecture provides a structural zero-hallucination guarantee and a three-layer continual-learning mechanism. All data and code are released.

</details>


### [119] [ProtLingo: Efficient Protein Language Modeling via Conditional Memory and Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04793)
*Mingrui Li,Sixian Shen,Minzhang Li,Ruiyi Zhang,Kexin Zhang,Jiakai Zhang,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtLingo 是一种高效的蛋白质语言模型框架，通过条件局部内存和稀疏专家路由增强预训练的单序列骨干网络，在 1.5 亿参数规模下实现了具有竞争力的蛋白质适应度预测、FLIP 基准和监督接触预测性能，同时保持了强参数效率和长程结构表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
